   14 | import type { SessionRuntimePayload } from "@/app/home/sessionRuntimePayload";
   15 | import { getAlbumBySlug } from "@/lib/albums";
   16 | 
   17 | export default async function SessionRuntime(props: {
   18 |   // When present, this is the “player album” canonical slug for /album/:slug routes.
   19 |   albumSlugOverride?: string | null;
   20 |   featuredAlbumSlug?: string | null;
   21 |   initialPortalTabId?: string | null;
   22 |   initialExegesisDisplayId?: string | null;
   23 | }) {
   24 |   // Important: this file is now a route-payload loader only.
   25 |   // It must not directly instantiate the persistent session shell.
   26 |   const selectedAlbumSlug =
   27 |     (props.albumSlugOverride ?? "").trim() ||
   28 |     (props.featuredAlbumSlug ?? "").trim() ||
   29 |     "god-defend";
   30 | 
   31 |   // Catalogue data is independent of Clerk and member provisioning. Start it
   32 |   // immediately so it can overlap the authenticated runtime path.
   33 |   const bundlePromise = getAlbumBySlug(selectedAlbumSlug);
   34 | 
   35 |   const { userId } = await auth();
   36 |   const user = userId ? await currentUser() : null;
   37 |   const email =
   38 |     user?.primaryEmailAddress?.emailAddress ??
   39 |     user?.emailAddresses?.[0]?.emailAddress ??
   40 |     null;
   41 | 
   42 |   let member: null | { id: string; created: boolean; email: string } = null;
   43 |   let entitlementKeys: string[] = [];
   44 |   let tier: Tier = "none";
   45 |   let memberSummary: PortalMemberSummary = emptyPortalMemberSummary();
   46 | 
   47 |   if (userId && email) {
   48 |     const ensured = await ensureMemberByClerk({
   49 |       clerkUserId: userId,
   50 |       email,
   51 |       source: "session_runtime_clerk",
   52 |       sourceDetail: { route: "(session)" },
   53 |     });
   54 | 
   55 |     member = { id: ensured.id, created: ensured.created, email };
   56 | 
   57 |     const [nextEntitlementKeys, nextMemberSummary] = await Promise.all([
   58 |       listCurrentEntitlementKeys(ensured.id),
   59 |       buildPortalMemberSummary(ensured.id),
   60 |     ]);
   61 | 
   62 |     entitlementKeys = nextEntitlementKeys;
   63 |     tier = deriveTier(entitlementKeys);
   64 |     memberSummary = nextMemberSummary;
   65 |   }
   66 | 
   67 |   const bundle = await bundlePromise;
   68 | 
   69 |   const payload: SessionRuntimePayload = {
   70 |     memberId: member?.id ?? null,
   71 |     entitlementKeys,
   72 |     memberSummary,
   73 |     initialPortalTabId: props.initialPortalTabId ?? null,
   74 |     initialExegesisDisplayId: props.initialExegesisDisplayId ?? null,
   75 |     bundle,
   76 |     tier,
   77 |     canManageBilling: !!member,
   78 |   };
   79 | 
   80 |   const routeKey = JSON.stringify({
   81 |     selectedAlbumSlug: bundle.albumSlug,
   82 |     initialPortalTabId: props.initialPortalTabId ?? null,
   83 |     initialExegesisDisplayId: props.initialExegesisDisplayId ?? null,
   84 |   });
   85 | 
   86 |   return <SessionRuntimePayloadBridge routeKey={routeKey} payload={payload} />;
   87 | }

    1 | // web/app/(site)/PlayerHost.tsx

    4 | import React from "react";

    8 | import StageInlineHost from "@/app/home/player/StageInlineHost";
    9 | 
   10 | export default function PlayerHost({
   11 |   children,
   12 | }: {
   13 |   children: React.ReactNode;
   14 | }) {
   15 |   React.useEffect(() => {
   16 |     if (
   17 |       typeof window === "undefined" ||
   18 |       process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_NAV_DEBUG !== "1"
   19 |     ) {
   20 |       return;
   21 |     }
   22 | 
   23 |     const tag = "[NAV]";
   24 |     const origPush = window.history.pushState.bind(window.history);
   25 |     const origReplace = window.history.replaceState.bind(window.history);
   26 | 
   27 |     const log = (
   28 |       kind: "pushState" | "replaceState",
   29 |       url?: string | URL | null,
   30 |     ) => {
   31 |       try {
   32 |         const href = String(url ?? "");
   33 |         const from = window.location.pathname;
   34 | 
   35 |         const toPath = href.startsWith("http")
   36 |           ? new URL(href).pathname
   37 |           : (href.split("?")[0] || "").trim();
   38 | 
   39 |         if (
   40 |           (from === "/portal" && toPath === "/journal") ||
   41 |           href.includes("/journal") ||
   42 |           href.includes("/portal") ||
   43 |           // legacy (avoid breaking old in-flight links/history)
   44 |           href.includes("/posts") ||
   45 |           href.includes("/extras")
   46 |         ) {
   47 |           console.log(tag, kind, { from, href }, new Error().stack);
   48 |         }
   49 |       } catch {
   50 |         // ignore
   51 |       }
   52 |     };
   53 | 
   54 |     window.history.pushState = function (
   55 |       data: unknown,
   56 |       unused: string,
   57 |       url?: string | URL | null,
   58 |     ): void {
   59 |       log("pushState", url);
   60 |       origPush(data, unused, url);
   61 |     };
   62 | 
   63 |     window.history.replaceState = function (
   64 |       data: unknown,
   65 |       unused: string,
   66 |       url?: string | URL | null,
   67 |     ): void {
   68 |       log("replaceState", url);
   69 |       origReplace(data, unused, url);
   70 |     };
   71 | 
   72 |     const onPop = () => {
   73 |       console.log(tag, "popstate", { href: window.location.href });
   74 |     };
   75 |     window.addEventListener("popstate", onPop);
   76 | 
   77 |     return () => {
   78 |       window.history.pushState = origPush;
   79 |       window.history.replaceState = origReplace;
   80 |       window.removeEventListener("popstate", onPop);
   81 |     };
   82 |   }, []);
   83 | 
   84 |   return (
   85 |     <PlayerStateProvider>
   86 |       <AudioEngine />
   87 |       <StageInlineHost />
   88 |       <TrackTitleSync fallbackLeaf="God Defend" mode="track" />
   89 |       {children}
   90 |     </PlayerStateProvider>
   91 |   );
   92 | }

   79 | function baseJson<T extends Record<string, unknown>>(
   80 |   body: T,
   81 |   opts: { correlationId: string; status?: number; anonId?: string },
   82 | ) {
   83 |   const res = NextResponse.json(body, { status: opts.status ?? 200 });
   84 |   // ✅ micro-cache to absorb double-invokes / fast remounts
   85 |   // safe because access state doesn't change multiple times per second.
   86 |   res.headers.set(
   87 |     "Cache-Control",
   88 |     "private, max-age=2, stale-while-revalidate=20",
   89 |   );
   90 |   res.headers.set("Vary", "Cookie");
   91 |   res.headers.set("x-correlation-id", opts.correlationId);
   92 |   if (opts.anonId) persistAnonId(res, opts.anonId);
   93 |   return res;
   94 | }

  131 | async function resolveAnonymousAccess(
  132 |   req: NextRequest,
  133 |   context: AccessRequestContext,
  134 | ): Promise<NextResponse> {
  135 |   const { albumId, albumScopeId, shareToken, correlationId } = context;
  136 |   const { anonId } = ensureAnonId(req);
  137 | 
  138 |   const policy = await getAlbumPolicyByAlbumId(albumId);
  139 |   const releaseAt = policy?.releaseAt ?? null;
  140 |   const embargoed = isEmbargoed(policy);
  141 | 
  142 |   if (embargoed && !shareToken) {
  143 |     return anonJsonWithId(
  144 |       anonId,
  145 |       {
  146 |         ok: true,
  147 |         allowed: false,
  148 |         embargoed: true,
  149 |         releaseAt,
  150 |         code: "EMBARGO",
  151 |         action: "wait" satisfies Action,
  152 |         reason: "This album is not released yet.",
  153 |         correlationId,
  154 |         redeemed: null,
  155 |       },
  156 |       { correlationId },
  157 |     );
  158 |   }
  159 | 
  160 |   if (shareToken) {
  161 |     const validation = await validateShareToken({
  162 |       token: shareToken,
  163 |       expectedScopeId: albumScopeId,
  164 |       anonId,
  165 |       resourceKind: "album",
  166 |       resourceId: albumScopeId,
  167 |       action: "access",
  168 |     });
  169 | 
  170 |     if (!validation.ok) {
  171 |       return anonJsonWithId(
  172 |         anonId,
  173 |         {
  174 |           ok: true,
  175 |           allowed: false,
  176 |           embargoed,
  177 |           releaseAt,
  178 |           code: validation.code,
  179 |           action: "login" as const,
  180 |           reason:
  181 |             validation.code === "CAP_REACHED"
  182 |               ? "Share link cap reached."
  183 |               : "Invalid or expired share token.",
  184 |           correlationId,
  185 |           redeemed: { ok: false, code: validation.code },
  186 |         },
  187 |         { correlationId },
  188 |       );
  189 |     }
  190 |

  198 |         })

  200 |

  275 |   correlationId: string,
  276 | ): Promise<NextResponse | null> {
  277 |   const debug = await readAdminDebugCookie();
  278 |   if (!debug) return null;
  279 | 
  280 |   const isAdmin = (
  281 |     await checkAccess(
  282 |       memberId,
  283 |       { kind: "global", required: [ENTITLEMENTS.ADMIN] },
  284 |       { log: false },
  285 |     )
  286 |   ).allowed;
  287 | 
  288 |   if (!isAdmin) return null;
  289 | 
  290 |   const force = String(debug.force ?? "none");
  291 | 
  292 |   switch (force) {
  293 |     case "AUTH_REQUIRED":
  294 |       return baseJson(
  295 |         {
  296 |           ok: true,
  297 |           allowed: false,
  298 |           embargoed: false,
  299 |           releaseAt: null,
  300 |           code: "AUTH_REQUIRED",
  301 |           action: "login",
  302 |           reason: "Sign in required",
  303 |           correlationId,
  304 |           redeemed: null,
  305 |         },
  306 |         { correlationId },
  307 |       );
  308 | 
  309 |     case "ENTITLEMENT_REQUIRED":
  310 |       return baseJson(
  311 |         {
  312 |           ok: true,
  313 |           allowed: false,
  314 |           embargoed: false,
  315 |           releaseAt: null,
  316 |           code: "ENTITLEMENT_REQUIRED",
  317 |           action: "subscribe",
  318 |           reason: "Entitlement required",
  319 |           correlationId,
  320 |           redeemed: null,
  321 |         },
  322 |         { correlationId },
  323 |       );
  324 | 
  325 |     case "ANON_CAP_REACHED":
  326 |       return baseJson(
  327 |         {
  328 |           ok: true,
  329 |           allowed: false,
  330 |           embargoed: false,
  331 |           releaseAt: null,
  332 |           code: "ANON_CAP_REACHED",
  333 |           action: "login",
  334 |           reason: "Anon cap reached",
  335 |           correlationId,
  336 |           redeemed: null,
  337 |         },
  338 |         { correlationId },
  339 |       );
  340 | 
  341 |     case "EMBARGOED":
  342 |       return baseJson(
  343 |         {
  344 |           ok: true,
  345 |           allowed: false,
  346 |           embargoed: true,
  347 |           releaseAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  348 |           code: "EMBARGOED",
  349 |           action: "wait",
  350 |           reason: "Embargoed",
  351 |           correlationId,
  352 |           redeemed: null,
  353 |         },
  354 |         { correlationId },
  355 |       );
  356 | 
  357 |     default:
  358 |       return null;
  359 |   }
  360 | }
  361 | 
  362 | async function resolveMemberShareState(params: {
  363 |   memberId: string;
  364 |   albumScopeId: string;
  365 |   shareToken: string;
  366 | }): Promise<MemberShareState> {
  367 |   if (!params.shareToken) {
  368 |     return {
  369 |       redeemed: null,
  370 |       shareTokenAllowsAccess: false,
  371 |       shareTokenAccess: null,
  372 |       sharePlaybackContext: null,
  373 |     };
  374 |   }
  375 | 
  376 |   const redemption = await redeemShareTokenForMember({
  377 |     token: params.shareToken,
  378 |     memberId: params.memberId,
  379 |     expectedScopeId: params.albumScopeId,
  380 |     resourceKind: "album",
  381 |     resourceId: params.albumScopeId,
  382 |     action: "redeem",
  383 |   });
  384 | 
  385 |   if (!redemption.ok) {
  386 |     return {
  387 |       redeemed: { ok: false, code: redemption.code },
  388 |       shareTokenAllowsAccess: false,
  389 |       shareTokenAccess: null,
  390 |       sharePlaybackContext: null,
  391 |     };
  392 |   }
  393 | 
  394 |   const sharePlaybackContext = redemption.telemetryLabel
  395 |     ? issueShareTokenPlaybackContext({
  396 |         shareTokenId: redemption.tokenId,
  397 |         scopeId: params.albumScopeId,
  398 |         tokenExpiresAt: redemption.shareTokenAccess.expiresAt,
  399 |         memberId: params.memberId,
  400 |         anonId: null,
  401 |       })
  402 |     : null;
  403 | 
  404 |   return {
  405 |     redeemed: { ok: true },
  406 |     shareTokenAllowsAccess: true,
  407 |     shareTokenAccess: redemption.shareTokenAccess,
  408 |     sharePlaybackContext,
  409 |   };
  410 | }
  411 | 
  412 | async function resolveMemberEmbargoGate(params: {
  413 |   memberId: string;
  414 |   albumScopeId: string;
  415 |   policy: AlbumPolicy | null;
  416 |   embargoed: boolean;
  417 |   releaseAt: string | null;
  418 |   shareTokenAllowsAccess: boolean;
  419 |   correlationId: string;
  420 |   redeemed: RedeemedState;
  421 | }): Promise<NextResponse | null> {
  422 |   if (!params.embargoed || params.shareTokenAllowsAccess) {
  423 |     return null;
  424 |   }
  425 | 
  426 |   const shareGrant = await checkAccess(
  427 |     params.memberId,
  428 |     {
  429 |       kind: "album",
  430 |       albumScopeId: params.albumScopeId,
  431 |       required: [ENTITLEMENTS.ALBUM_SHARE_GRANT],
  432 |     },
  433 |     {
  434 |       log: true,
  435 |       action: ACCESS_ACTIONS.ACCESS_CHECK,
  436 |       correlationId: params.correlationId,
  437 |     },
  438 |   );
  439 | 
  440 |   if (shareGrant.allowed) {
  441 |     return null;
  442 |   }
  443 | 
  444 |   const { policy } = params;
  445 | 
  446 |   if (policy?.earlyAccessEnabled && policy.earlyAccessTiers.length > 0) {
  447 |     const keys = await listCurrentEntitlementKeys(params.memberId);
  448 |     const keySet = new Set(keys);
  449 |     const allowedTierKeys = policy.earlyAccessTiers.map(
  450 |       (tier) => `tier_${tier}`,
  451 |     );
  452 | 
  453 |     if (allowedTierKeys.some((key) => keySet.has(key))) {
  454 |       return null;
  455 |     }
  456 | 
  457 |     return baseJson(
  458 |       {
  459 |         ok: true,
  460 |         allowed: false,
  461 |         embargoed: true,
  462 |         releaseAt: params.releaseAt,
  463 |         code: "EMBARGO",
  464 |         action: "subscribe" satisfies Action,
  465 |         reason: "This album is not released yet. Upgrade for early access.",
  466 |         correlationId: params.correlationId,
  467 |         redeemed: params.redeemed,
  468 |       },
  469 |       { correlationId: params.correlationId },
  470 |     );
  471 |   }
  472 | 
  473 |   return baseJson(
  474 |     {
  475 |       ok: true,
  476 |       allowed: false,
  477 |       embargoed: true,
  478 |       releaseAt: params.releaseAt,
  479 |       code: "EMBARGO",
  480 |       action: "wait" satisfies Action,
  481 |       reason: "This album is not released yet.",
  482 |       correlationId: params.correlationId,
  483 |       redeemed: params.redeemed,
  484 |     },
  485 |     { correlationId: params.correlationId },
  486 |   );
  487 | }
  488 | 
  489 | async function resolveMemberTierGate(params: {
  490 |   memberId: string;
  491 |   policy: AlbumPolicy | null;
  492 |   releaseAt: string | null;
  493 |   correlationId: string;
  494 |   redeemed: RedeemedState;
  495 | }): Promise<NextResponse | null> {
  496 |   const minTier = params.policy?.minTierForPlayback;
  497 | 
  498 |   if (!minTier) {
  499 |     return null;
  500 |   }
  501 | 
  502 |   const keys = await listCurrentEntitlementKeys(params.memberId);
  503 |   const keySet = new Set(keys);
  504 |   const requiredTierKeys = tierAtOrAbove(minTier);
  505 | 
  506 |   if (requiredTierKeys.some((key) => keySet.has(key))) {
  507 |     return null;
  508 |   }
  509 | 
  510 |   return baseJson(
  511 |     {
  512 |       ok: true,
  513 |       allowed: false,
  514 |       embargoed: false,
  515 |       releaseAt: params.releaseAt,
  516 |       code: "TIER_REQUIRED",
  517 |       action: "subscribe" satisfies Action,
  518 |       reason: `This album requires ${minTier} tier or higher.`,
  519 |       correlationId: params.correlationId,
  520 |       redeemed: params.redeemed,
  521 |     },
  522 |     { correlationId: params.correlationId },
  523 |   );
  524 | }
  525 | 
  526 | async function resolveMemberAccess(params: {
  527 |   memberId: string;
  528 |   albumId: string;
  529 |   albumScopeId: string;
  530 |   correlationId: string;
  531 |   shareState: MemberShareState;
  532 | }): Promise<NextResponse> {
  533 |   const policy = await getAlbumPolicyByAlbumId(params.albumId);
  534 |   const releaseAt = policy?.releaseAt ?? null;
  535 |   const embargoed = isEmbargoed(policy);
  536 | 
  537 |   const embargoResponse = await resolveMemberEmbargoGate({
  538 |     memberId: params.memberId,
  539 |     albumScopeId: params.albumScopeId,
  540 |     policy,
  541 |     embargoed,
  542 |     releaseAt,
  543 |     shareTokenAllowsAccess: params.shareState.shareTokenAllowsAccess,
  544 |     correlationId: params.correlationId,
  545 |     redeemed: params.shareState.redeemed,
  546 |   });
  547 | 
  548 |   if (embargoResponse) {
  549 |     return embargoResponse;
  550 |   }
  551 | 
  552 |   const tierResponse = await resolveMemberTierGate({
  553 |     memberId: params.memberId,
  554 |     policy,
  555 |     releaseAt,
  556 |     correlationId: params.correlationId,
  557 |     redeemed: params.shareState.redeemed,
  558 |   });
  559 | 
  560 |   if (tierResponse) {
  561 |     return tierResponse;
  562 |   }
  563 | 
  564 |   const decision = await checkAccess(
  565 |     params.memberId,
  566 |     {
  567 |       kind: "album",
  568 |       albumScopeId: params.albumScopeId,
  569 |       required: [ENTITLEMENTS.PLAY_ALBUM],
  570 |     },
  571 |     {
  572 |       log: true,
  573 |       action: ACCESS_ACTIONS.ACCESS_CHECK,
  574 |       correlationId: params.correlationId,
  575 |     },
  576 |   );
  577 | 
  578 |   const allowed = Boolean(
  579 |     decision.allowed || params.shareState.shareTokenAllowsAccess,
  580 |   );
  581 | 
  582 |   return baseJson(
  583 |     {
  584 |       ok: true,
  585 |       allowed,
  586 |       embargoed: embargoed && !allowed,
  587 |       releaseAt,
  588 |       code: allowed ? null : "NO_ENTITLEMENT",
  589 |       action: allowed ? null : ("subscribe" satisfies Action),
  590 |       reason: allowed ? null : "reason" in decision ? decision.reason : null,
  591 |       correlationId: params.correlationId,
  592 |       redeemed: params.shareState.redeemed,
  593 |       shareTokenAccess:
  594 |         allowed && params.shareState.shareTokenAllowsAccess
  595 |           ? params.shareState.shareTokenAccess
  596 |           : null,
  597 |       sharePlaybackContext:
  598 |         allowed && params.shareState.shareTokenAllowsAccess
  599 |           ? params.shareState.sharePlaybackContext
  600 |           : null,
  601 |       sharePlaybackScopeId:
  602 |         allowed &&
  603 |         params.shareState.shareTokenAllowsAccess &&
  604 |         params.shareState.sharePlaybackContext
  605 |           ? params.albumScopeId
  606 |           : null,
  607 |     },
  608 |     { correlationId: params.correlationId },
  609 |   );
  610 | }
  611 | 
  612 | export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  613 |   const correlationId = newCorrelationId();
  614 |   const { userId } = await auth();
  615 | 
  616 |   const url = new URL(req.url);
  617 |   const albumId = normalizeAlbumId(
  618 |     (url.searchParams.get("albumId") ?? "").trim(),
  619 |   );
  620 |   const shareToken = (
  621 |     url.searchParams.get("st") ??
  622 |     url.searchParams.get("share") ??
  623 |     ""
  624 |   ).trim();
  625 | 
  626 |   if (!albumId) {
  627 |     return baseJson(
  628 |       {
  629 |         ok: true,
  630 |         allowed: false,
  631 |         embargoed: false,
  632 |         releaseAt: null,
  633 |         code: "INVALID_REQUEST",
  634 |         action: null,
  635 |         reason: "Missing albumId",
  636 |         correlationId,
  637 |         redeemed: null,
  638 |       },
  639 |       { correlationId },
  640 |     );
  641 |   }
  642 | 
  643 |   const albumScopeId = `alb:${albumId}`;
  644 | 
  645 |   if (!userId) {
  646 |     return resolveAnonymousAccess(req, {
  647 |       albumId,
  648 |       albumScopeId,
  649 |       shareToken,
  650 |       correlationId,
  651 |     });
  652 |   }
  653 | 
  654 |   const memberId = await getMemberIdByClerkUserId(userId);
  655 | 
  656 |   if (!memberId) {
  657 |     const anonForAuthed = shareToken ? ensureAnonId(req).anonId : undefined;
  658 | 
  659 |     return baseJson(
  660 |       {
  661 |         ok: true,
  662 |         allowed: false,
  663 |         embargoed: false,
  664 |         releaseAt: null,
  665 |         code: "PROVISIONING",
  666 |         action: "wait" satisfies Action,
  667 |         reason: "Member profile is still being created",
  668 |         correlationId,
  669 |         redeemed: null,
  670 |       },
  671 |       { correlationId, anonId: anonForAuthed },
  672 |     );
  673 |   }
  674 | 
  675 |   const debugResponse = await resolveAdminDebugOverride(
  676 |     memberId,
  677 |     correlationId,
  678 |   );
  679 | 
  680 |   if (debugResponse) {
  681 |     return debugResponse;
  682 |   }
  683 | 
  684 |   const shareState = await resolveMemberShareState({
  685 |     memberId,
  686 |     albumScopeId,
  687 |     shareToken,
  688 |   });
  689 | 
  690 |   return resolveMemberAccess({
  691 |     memberId,
  692 |     albumId,
  693 |     albumScopeId,
  694 |     correlationId,
  695 |     shareState,
  696 |   });
  697 | }

  107 | async function resolveAlbumSessionSample(params: {
  108 |   req: NextRequest;
  109 |   access: MuxPlaybackAccessContext;
  110 |   albumId: string;
  111 |   requestedPlaybackId: string;
  112 |   tracks: AlbumPlaybackSessionAsset[];
  113 |   correlationId: string;
  114 | }): Promise<AlbumSessionSampleSelection> {
  115 |   if (params.access.userId || params.access.tokenAllowsPlayback) {
  116 |     return {
  117 |       ok: true,
  118 |       mode: "full",
  119 |       sampleSession: null,
  120 |       tracksToSign: params.tracks,
  121 |     };
  122 |   }
  123 | 
  124 |   const sample = await resolveAnonPlaybackSample({
  125 |     anonId: params.access.anonId,
  126 |     albumId: params.albumId,
  127 |     requestedPlaybackId: params.requestedPlaybackId,
  128 |     tracks: params.tracks.map((track) => ({
  129 |       recordingId: track.recordingId,
  130 |       playbackId: track.playbackId,
  131 |     })),
  132 |   });
  133 | 
  134 |   if (!sample.ok) {
  135 |     const invalidStart = sample.reason === "invalid_start";
  136 | 
  137 |     return {
  138 |       ok: false,
  139 |       response: muxPlaybackGateError(params.req, {
  140 |         correlationId: params.correlationId,
  141 |         status: invalidStart ? 400 : 403,
  142 |         code: invalidStart ? "INVALID_REQUEST" : "PLAYBACK_CAP_REACHED",
  143 |         action: invalidStart ? "wait" : "login",
  144 |         message: invalidStart
  145 |           ? "Missing or invalid anonymous sample starting track."
  146 |           : "Enter your email address to continue listening.",
  147 |         anonId: params.access.anonId,
  148 |         isNewAnonId: params.access.isNewAnonId,
  149 |       }),
  150 |     };
  151 |   }
  152 | 
  153 |   const assetsByPlaybackId = new Map(
  154 |     params.tracks.map((track) => [track.playbackId, track]),
  155 |   );
  156 | 
  157 |   const boundedTracks = sample.sample.tracks
  158 |     .map((track) => assetsByPlaybackId.get(track.playbackId) ?? null)
  159 |     .filter(
  160 |       (track): track is AlbumPlaybackSessionAsset => track !== null,
  161 |     );
  162 | 
  163 |   if (boundedTracks.length !== sample.sample.tracks.length) {
  164 |     return {
  165 |       ok: false,
  166 |       response: muxPlaybackGateError(params.req, {
  167 |         correlationId: params.correlationId,
  168 |         status: 500,
  169 |         code: "INVALID_REQUEST",
  170 |         action: "wait",
  171 |         message: "Anonymous sample session could not be resolved.",
  172 |         anonId: params.access.anonId,
  173 |         isNewAnonId: params.access.isNewAnonId,
  174 |       }),
  175 |     };
  176 |   }
  177 | 
  178 |   return {
  179 |     ok: true,
  180 |     mode: "sample",
  181 |     sampleSession: sample.sample,
  182 |     tracksToSign: boundedTracks,
  183 |   };
  184 | }

  186 | async function signAlbumSessionTracks(
  187 |   tracks: readonly AlbumPlaybackSessionAsset[],
  188 |   signer: MuxPlaybackSigner,
  189 | ): Promise<AlbumSessionTrackToken[]> {
  190 |   return Promise.all(
  191 |     tracks.map(async (track) => ({
  192 |       recordingId: track.recordingId,
  193 |       displayId: track.displayId,
  194 |       playbackId: track.playbackId,
  195 |       token: await signer.sign(track.playbackId),
  196 |       expiresAt: signer.expiresAt,
  197 |     })),
  198 |   );
  199 | }

  201 | export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  202 |   const correlationId = correlationIdFromRequest(req);
  203 |   const body = await readAlbumSessionRequest(req);
  204 |   const requestedAlbumId = normalizeMuxAlbumId(body?.albumId);
  205 | 
  206 |   if (!requestedAlbumId) {
  207 |     return muxPlaybackGateError(req, {
  208 |       correlationId,
  209 |       status: 400,
  210 |       code: "INVALID_REQUEST",
  211 |       action: "wait",
  212 |       message: "Missing albumId",
  213 |     });
  214 |   }
  215 | 
  216 |   logAlbumSessionRequested(req, requestedAlbumId);
  217 | 
  218 |   const sessionAssets = await getAlbumPlaybackAssetsForSession({
  219 |     albumId: requestedAlbumId,
  220 |   });
  221 | 
  222 |   if (
  223 |     !sessionAssets.ok ||
  224 |     !sessionAssets.albumId ||
  225 |     !sessionAssets.albumScopeId
  226 |   ) {
  227 |     return muxPlaybackGateError(req, {
  228 |       correlationId,
  229 |       status: 404,
  230 |       code: "INVALID_REQUEST",
  231 |       action: "wait",
  232 |       message: "No playable tracks were found for this album.",
  233 |     });
  234 |   }
  235 | 
  236 |   const accessResult = await resolveMuxPlaybackAccess(req, {
  237 |     albumId: sessionAssets.albumId,
  238 |     albumScopeId: sessionAssets.albumScopeId,
  239 |     shareToken: readMuxShareToken(req, body?.st),
  240 |     correlationId,
  241 |   });
  242 | 
  243 |   if (!accessResult.ok) {
  244 |     return accessResult.response;
  245 |   }
  246 | 
  247 |   const sampleSelection = await resolveAlbumSessionSample({
  248 |     req,
  249 |     access: accessResult.context,
  250 |     albumId: sessionAssets.albumId,
  251 |     requestedPlaybackId:
  252 |       typeof body?.startPlaybackId === "string"
  253 |         ? body.startPlaybackId.trim()
  254 |         : "",
  255 |     tracks: sessionAssets.tracks,
  256 |     correlationId,
  257 |   });
  258 | 
  259 |   if (!sampleSelection.ok) {
  260 |     return sampleSelection.response;
  261 |   }
  262 | 
  263 |   const sampleExpiresAtSeconds = sampleSelection.sampleSession
  264 |     ? Math.floor(sampleSelection.sampleSession.expiresAt.getTime() / 1000)
  265 |     : null;
  266 | 
  267 |   const signer = await createMuxPlaybackSigner({
  268 |     baseTtlSeconds: Number(
  269 |       process.env.MUX_ALBUM_SESSION_TOKEN_TTL_SECONDS ??
  270 |         process.env.MUX_TOKEN_TTL_SECONDS ??
  271 |         7200,
  272 |     ),
  273 |     sampleExpiresAtSeconds,
  274 |   });
  275 | 
  276 |   const tracks = await signAlbumSessionTracks(
  277 |     sampleSelection.tracksToSign,
  278 |     signer,
  279 |   );
  280 | 
  281 |   logAlbumSessionIssued({
  282 |     albumId: sessionAssets.albumId,
  283 |     trackCount: tracks.length,
  284 |     expiresAt: signer.expiresAt,
  285 |     correlationId,
  286 |   });
  287 | 
  288 |   const out: AlbumSessionOk = {
  289 |     ok: true,
  290 |     albumId: sessionAssets.albumId,
  291 |     expiresAt: signer.expiresAt,
  292 |     tracks,
  293 |     mode: sampleSelection.mode,
  294 |     ...(sampleSelection.sampleSession
  295 |       ? {
  296 |           sampleSession: {
  297 |             id: sampleSelection.sampleSession.id,
  298 |             expiresAt: signer.expiresAt,
  299 |             trackCount: tracks.length,
  300 |           },
  301 |         }
  302 |       : {}),
  303 |     correlationId,
  304 |   };
  305 | 
  306 |   const response = jsonOk(out, { correlationId });
  307 |   persistMuxAnonIdentity(response, accessResult.context);
  308 |   return response;
  309 | }

===== COMPLETE FILE: app/api/playback/live/route.ts =====
    1 | import "server-only";
    2 | 
    3 | import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";

    5 |   clampActiveListenerWindowSeconds,
    6 |   getSiteActiveListenerCount,
    7 | } from "@/lib/playbackLive";
    8 | 
    9 | function parseWindowSeconds(value: string | null): number | undefined {
   10 |   if (!value) return undefined;
   11 | 
   12 |   const parsed = Number(value);
   13 |   if (!Number.isFinite(parsed)) return undefined;
   14 | 
   15 |   return Math.floor(parsed);
   16 | }
   17 | 
   18 | export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
   19 |   const requestedWindowSeconds = parseWindowSeconds(
   20 |     req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("windowSeconds"),
   21 |   );
   22 | 
   23 |   const windowSeconds =
   24 |     requestedWindowSeconds == null
   25 |       ? clampActiveListenerWindowSeconds()
   26 |       : clampActiveListenerWindowSeconds(requestedWindowSeconds);
   27 | 
   28 |   const activeListeners = await getSiteActiveListenerCount(windowSeconds);
   29 | 
   30 |   const res = NextResponse.json({
   31 |     ok: true,
   32 |     activeListeners,
   33 |     windowSeconds,
   34 |     generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
   35 |   });
   36 | 
   37 |   res.headers.set(
   38 |     "Cache-Control",
   39 |     "public, max-age=5, s-maxage=15, stale-while-revalidate=45",
   40 |   );
   41 | 
   42 |   return res;
   43 | }

===== COMPLETE FILE: app/home/gating/GateBroker.tsx =====
    1 | //web/app/home/gating/GateBroker.tsx
    2 | "use client";
    3 | 
    4 | import React from "react";
    5 | import type {
    6 |   GateAction,
    7 |   GateCodeRaw,
    8 |   GateDomain,
    9 |   GateReason,
   10 |   GateUiMode,
   11 | } from "@/app/home/gating/gateTypes";
   12 | import { canonicalizeLegacyCapCode } from "@/app/home/gating/gateTypes";
   13 | 
   14 | export type GateReport = {
   15 |   code: GateCodeRaw;
   16 |   action: GateAction;
   17 |   message: string;
   18 |   domain: GateDomain;
   19 |   uiMode: GateUiMode; // REQUIRED: adapters must ask engine
   20 |   correlationId?: string | null;
   21 | };
   22 | 
   23 | export type GateState = {
   24 |   active: GateReason | null;
   25 |   uiMode: GateUiMode;
   26 |   lastSetAtMs: number | null;
   27 | };
   28 | 
   29 | type Ctx = {
   30 |   gate: GateState;
   31 |   reportGate: (r: GateReport) => void;
   32 |   clearGate: (opts?: { domain?: GateDomain }) => void;
   33 | };
   34 | 
   35 | const GateBrokerContext = React.createContext<Ctx | null>(null);
   36 | 
   37 | export function GateBrokerProvider(props: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
   38 |   const [gate, setGate] = React.useState<GateState>({
   39 |     active: null,
   40 |     uiMode: "none",
   41 |     lastSetAtMs: null,
   42 |   });
   43 | 
   44 |   const reportGate = React.useCallback((r: GateReport) => {
   45 |     const normalized = canonicalizeLegacyCapCode(r.code, r.domain);
   46 | 
   47 |     setGate((prev) => {
   48 |       const nextReason: GateReason = {
   49 |         code: normalized,
   50 |         action: r.action,
   51 |         message: r.message,
   52 |         correlationId: r.correlationId ?? null,
   53 |         domain: r.domain,
   54 |       };
   55 | 
   56 |       // Idempotent-ish: don’t churn state if nothing meaningful changed.
   57 |       const prevActive = prev.active;
   58 |       const same =
   59 |         prevActive &&
   60 |         prevActive.code === nextReason.code &&
   61 |         prevActive.action === nextReason.action &&
   62 |         prevActive.message === nextReason.message &&
   63 |         (prevActive.correlationId ?? null) ===
   64 |           (nextReason.correlationId ?? null) &&
   65 |         (prevActive.domain ?? "generic") === (nextReason.domain ?? "generic");
   66 | 
   67 |       if (same) return prev;
   68 | 
   69 |       if (!r.uiMode) {
   70 |         // This should be impossible once adapters are spine-aligned.
   71 |         // Keep the crash dev-only so prod sessions don't brick if an edge path regresses.
   72 |         if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== "production") {
   73 |           throw new Error(
   74 |             `[GateBroker] Missing uiMode in reportGate (domain=${r.domain}, code=${normalized})`,
   75 |           );
   76 |         }
   77 |       }
   78 | 
   79 |       return {
   80 |         active: nextReason,
   81 |         uiMode: r.uiMode ?? "inline",
   82 |         lastSetAtMs: Date.now(),
   83 |       };
   84 |     });
   85 |   }, []);
   86 | 
   87 |   const clearGate = React.useCallback((opts?: { domain?: GateDomain }) => {
   88 |     setGate((prev) => {
   89 |       if (!prev.active) return prev;
   90 |       if (opts?.domain && (prev.active.domain ?? "generic") !== opts.domain)
   91 |         return prev;
   92 |       return { active: null, uiMode: "none", lastSetAtMs: prev.lastSetAtMs };
   93 |     });
   94 |   }, []);
   95 | 
   96 |   const value = React.useMemo<Ctx>(
   97 |     () => ({ gate, reportGate, clearGate }),
   98 |     [gate, reportGate, clearGate],
   99 |   );
  100 | 
  101 |   return (
  102 |     <GateBrokerContext.Provider value={value}>
  103 |       {props.children}
  104 |     </GateBrokerContext.Provider>
  105 |   );
  106 | }
  107 | 
  108 | export function useGateBroker(): Ctx {
  109 |   const ctx = React.useContext(GateBrokerContext);
  110 |   if (!ctx) {
  111 |     throw new Error("useGateBroker must be used within GateBrokerProvider");
  112 |   }
  113 |   return ctx;
  114 | }

===== COMPLETE FILE: app/home/gating/fromPayload.ts =====
    1 | // web/app/home/gating/fromPayload.ts
    2 | import { gate } from "@/app/home/gating/gate";
    3 | import type {
    4 |   GateAttempt,
    5 |   GateContext,
    6 |   GateResult,
    7 | } from "@/app/home/gating/gate";
    8 | import type { GatePayload, GateDomain } from "@/app/home/gating/gateTypes";
    9 | import { canonicalizeLegacyCapCode } from "@/app/home/gating/gateTypes";
   10 | 
   11 | /** Narrow unknown -> GatePayload (bare or wrapped). */
   12 | function extractGatePayload(raw: unknown): GatePayload | null {
   13 |   if (!raw || typeof raw !== "object") return null;
   14 | 
   15 |   // Wrapped: { ok:false; gate:{...} }
   16 |   const r = raw as Record<string, unknown>;
   17 |   if (r.ok === false && "gate" in r) {
   18 |     const g = r.gate as unknown;
   19 |     if (g && typeof g === "object") {
   20 |       const gg = g as Record<string, unknown>;
   21 |       if (
   22 |         typeof gg.code === "string" &&
   23 |         typeof gg.action === "string" &&
   24 |         typeof gg.domain === "string" &&
   25 |         typeof gg.message === "string"
   26 |       ) {
   27 |         return g as GatePayload;
   28 |       }
   29 |     }
   30 |   }
   31 | 
   32 |   // Bare payload: { code, action, domain, message, ... }
   33 |   if (
   34 |     typeof r.code === "string" &&
   35 |     typeof r.action === "string" &&
   36 |     typeof r.domain === "string" &&
   37 |     typeof r.message === "string"
   38 |   ) {
   39 |     return raw as GatePayload;
   40 |   }
   41 | 
   42 |   return null;
   43 | }
   44 | 
   45 | /** Canonicalize legacy CAP_* to domain-specific cap codes, plus hygiene. */
   46 | function normalizePayload(payload: GatePayload): GatePayload {
   47 |   const domain = (payload.domain ?? "unknown") as GateDomain;
   48 |   const code = canonicalizeLegacyCapCode(payload.code, domain);
   49 | 
   50 |   return {
   51 |     ...payload,
   52 |     domain,
   53 |     code,
   54 |     message: (payload.message ?? "").trim(),
   55 |     correlationId: payload.correlationId ?? null,
   56 |   };
   57 | }
   58 | 
   59 | function ctxFromCode(
   60 |   codeRaw: GatePayload["code"],
   61 |   domain: GatePayload["domain"],
   62 | ) {
   63 |   const code = canonicalizeLegacyCapCode(codeRaw, domain);
   64 | 
   65 |   return {
   66 |     readReceiptsCapReached: code === "READ_RECEIPTS_CAP_REACHED",
   67 |     playbackCapReached: code === "PLAYBACK_CAP_REACHED",
   68 |     journalReadCapReached: code === "JOURNAL_READ_CAP_REACHED",
   69 |     exegesisThreadReadCapReached: code === "EXEGESIS_THREAD_READ_CAP_REACHED",
   70 |     isEmbargoed: code === "EMBARGO",
   71 |     isProvisioning: code === "PROVISIONING",
   72 |     // tier/entitlement are “negative capability flags”
   73 |     hasTierAccess: code === "TIER_REQUIRED" ? false : undefined,
   74 |     hasEntitlement: code === "ENTITLEMENT_REQUIRED" ? false : undefined,
   75 |   } as const;
   76 | }
   77 | 
   78 | /**
   79 |  * Compute UI policy (uiMode + CTA) locally, based on gate() engine.
   80 |  * Server supplies only the reason (code/action/domain/message).
   81 |  *
   82 |  * This variant assumes you already have a GatePayload.
   83 |  */
   84 | export function gateResultFromPayload(opts: {
   85 |   payload: GatePayload;
   86 |   attempt: GateAttempt;
   87 |   isSignedIn?: boolean;
   88 |   intent: "passive" | "explicit";
   89 | }): GateResult {
   90 |   const payload = normalizePayload(opts.payload);
   91 | 
   92 |   const flags = ctxFromCode(payload.code, payload.domain);
   93 | 
   94 |   const ctx: GateContext = {
   95 |     isSignedIn: opts.isSignedIn ?? false,
   96 |     intent: opts.intent,
   97 |     ...flags,
   98 |   };
   99 | 
  100 |   const res = gate(opts.attempt, ctx);
  101 | 
  102 |   // Prefer server message when blocked (so copy can be server-authored),
  103 |   // but keep engine invariants for uiMode + CTA.
  104 |   if (!res.ok) {
  105 |     return {
  106 |       ...res,
  107 |       cta: {
  108 |         ...res.cta,
  109 |         action: payload.action || res.cta.action,
  110 |       },
  111 |       reason: {
  112 |         ...res.reason,
  113 |         // Keep engine code/domain/uiMode, but preserve server-authored action/message.
  114 |         action: payload.action || res.reason.action,
  115 |         message: payload.message || res.reason.message,
  116 |         correlationId: payload.correlationId ?? null,
  117 |       },
  118 |     };
  119 |   }
  120 | 
  121 |   return res;
  122 | }
  123 | 
  124 | /**
  125 |  * Convenience: accept unknown API JSON and (if it contains a gate) produce GateResult.
  126 |  * Returns null when no gate payload is present.
  127 |  */
  128 | export function gateResultFromUnknown(opts: {
  129 |   raw: unknown;
  130 |   attempt: GateAttempt;
  131 |   isSignedIn?: boolean;
  132 |   intent: "passive" | "explicit";
  133 | }): GateResult | null {
  134 |   const payload = extractGatePayload(opts.raw);
  135 |   if (!payload) return null;
  136 | 
  137 |   return gateResultFromPayload({
  138 |     payload,
  139 |     attempt: opts.attempt,
  140 |     isSignedIn: opts.isSignedIn,
  141 |     intent: opts.intent,
  142 |   });
  143 | }
  144 | 
  145 | /**
  146 |  * Convenience: extract + normalize just the payload (handy when you need to forward it).
  147 |  * Returns null when no gate payload is present.
  148 |  */
  149 | export function gatePayloadFromUnknown(raw: unknown): GatePayload | null {
  150 |   const payload = extractGatePayload(raw);
  151 |   return payload ? normalizePayload(payload) : null;
  152 | }

===== COMPLETE FILE: app/home/gating/gate.ts =====
    1 | //web/app/home/gating/gate.ts
    2 | import type {
    3 |   GateAction,
    4 |   GateCode,
    5 |   GateCodeRaw,
    6 |   GateDomain,
    7 |   GateReason,
    8 |   GateUiMode,
    9 | } from "@/app/home/gating/gateTypes";
   10 | import { canonicalizeLegacyCapCode } from "@/app/home/gating/gateTypes";
   11 | 
   12 | export type GateVerb =
   13 |   | "play"
   14 |   | "download"
   15 |   | "openComposer"
   16 |   | "postComment"
   17 |   | "vote"
   18 |   | "report"
   19 |   | "edit"
   20 |   | "claimName"
   21 |   | "readFullThread"
   22 |   | "markSeen";
   23 | 
   24 | export type GateAttempt = {
   25 |   verb: GateVerb;
   26 |   domain: GateDomain;
   27 | };
   28 | 
   29 | export type GateContext = {
   30 |   isSignedIn: boolean;
   31 | 
   32 |   /**
   33 |    * Whether this attempt was driven by explicit user intent (click/tap)
   34 |    * versus passive system behavior (prefetch, background hydration, queue priming).
   35 |    * Used for spotlight eligibility.
   36 |    */
   37 |   intent?: "passive" | "explicit";
   38 | 
   39 |   // optional capability flags (fill per-domain as you adopt)
   40 |   hasEntitlement?: boolean;
   41 |   hasTierAccess?: boolean;
   42 | 
   43 |   // lifecycle flags
   44 |   isEmbargoed?: boolean;
   45 |   isProvisioning?: boolean;
   46 | 
   47 |   // caps (domain-explicit)
   48 |   playbackCapReached?: boolean;
   49 |   readReceiptsCapReached?: boolean;
   50 |   journalReadCapReached?: boolean;
   51 |   exegesisThreadReadCapReached?: boolean;
   52 | };
   53 | 
   54 | export type GateOk = { ok: true };
   55 | 
   56 | export type GateBlocked = {
   57 |   ok: false;
   58 |   reason: GateReason & { code: GateCode };
   59 |   uiMode: GateUiMode;
   60 |   cta: { action: GateAction; label: string };
   61 | };
   62 | 
   63 | export type GateResult = GateOk | GateBlocked;
   64 | 
   65 | function defaultMessageFor(code: GateCode, verb: GateVerb): string {
   66 |   switch (code) {
   67 |     case "AUTH_REQUIRED":
   68 |       return "Sign in to continue.";
   69 |     case "ENTITLEMENT_REQUIRED":
   70 |       return verb === "download"
   71 |         ? "Downloads are for members."
   72 |         : "This is for members.";
   73 |     case "TIER_REQUIRED":
   74 |       return "Upgrade your membership to access this.";
   75 |     case "EMBARGO":
   76 |       return "This content isn’t available yet.";
   77 |     case "PROVISIONING":
   78 |       return "Still setting things up. Try again shortly.";
   79 |     case "READ_RECEIPTS_CAP_REACHED":
   80 |       return "You’ve reached the anonymous activity limit.";
   81 |     case "PLAYBACK_CAP_REACHED":
   82 |       return "Enter your email address to continue listening.";
   83 |     case "JOURNAL_READ_CAP_REACHED":
   84 |       return "You’ve reached the anonymous reading limit.";
   85 |     case "EXEGESIS_THREAD_READ_CAP_REACHED":
   86 |       return "Sign in to keep reading.";
   87 |     case "INVALID_REQUEST":
   88 |       return "That request couldn’t be processed.";
   89 |   }
   90 | }
   91 | 
   92 | function defaultActionFor(code: GateCode): GateAction {
   93 |   switch (code) {
   94 |     case "AUTH_REQUIRED":
   95 |     case "PLAYBACK_CAP_REACHED":
   96 |     case "EXEGESIS_THREAD_READ_CAP_REACHED":
   97 |       return "login";
   98 |     case "ENTITLEMENT_REQUIRED":
   99 |     case "TIER_REQUIRED":
  100 |     case "READ_RECEIPTS_CAP_REACHED":
  101 |     case "JOURNAL_READ_CAP_REACHED":
  102 |       return "subscribe";
  103 |     case "EMBARGO":
  104 |     case "PROVISIONING":
  105 |       return "wait";
  106 |     case "INVALID_REQUEST":
  107 |       return "wait";
  108 |   }
  109 | }
  110 | 
  111 | function defaultUiModeFor(
  112 |   domain: GateDomain,
  113 |   code: GateCode,
  114 |   verb: GateVerb,
  115 |   intent: "passive" | "explicit",
  116 | ): GateUiMode {
  117 |   // Invariants: receipts, journal, and exegesis read caps never spotlight (inline only).
  118 |   if (code === "READ_RECEIPTS_CAP_REACHED") return "inline";
  119 |   if (code === "JOURNAL_READ_CAP_REACHED") return "inline";
  120 |   if (code === "EXEGESIS_THREAD_READ_CAP_REACHED") return "inline";
  121 | 
  122 |   // Playback cap is the canonical blocking playback gate.
  123 |   // If a play attempt is being denied, surface the full-screen prompt even when
  124 |   // the denial arose from auto-advance rather than a fresh click/tap.
  125 |   if (
  126 |     domain === "playback" &&
  127 |     verb === "play" &&
  128 |     code === "PLAYBACK_CAP_REACHED"
  129 |   ) {
  130 |     return "spotlight";
  131 |   }
  132 | 
  133 |   // Primary intent blocks tend to be global (but non-spotlight).
  134 |   if (domain === "playback" && verb === "play") return "global";
  135 |   if (verb === "download") return "global";
  136 | 
  137 |   // Everything else defaults to inline (action-only gating).
  138 |   return "inline";
  139 | }
  140 | 
  141 | /**
  142 |  * Pure policy brain. No React, no side effects.
  143 |  * You can call this from adapters or from a future GateBroker.
  144 |  */
  145 | export function gate(attempt: GateAttempt, ctx: GateContext): GateResult {
  146 |   const intent = ctx.intent ?? "passive";
  147 | 
  148 |   // Verbs that are allowed for anonymous users (product invariant: “read stays broadly available”).
  149 |   // You can tighten/expand this list per module over time.
  150 |   const anonAllowedVerb: boolean =
  151 |     attempt.verb === "play" ||
  152 |     attempt.verb === "readFullThread" ||
  153 |     attempt.verb === "markSeen";
  154 | 
  155 |   // 1) Embargo / provisioning are domain-agnostic “wait” gates.
  156 |   if (ctx.isEmbargoed) {
  157 |     const code: GateCode = "EMBARGO";
  158 |     const action = defaultActionFor(code);
  159 |     return {
  160 |       ok: false,
  161 |       uiMode: defaultUiModeFor(attempt.domain, code, attempt.verb, intent),
  162 |       cta: { action, label: "Not yet" },
  163 |       reason: {
  164 |         code,
  165 |         action,
  166 |         message: defaultMessageFor(code, attempt.verb),
  167 |         domain: attempt.domain,
  168 |       },
  169 |     };
  170 |   }
  171 | 
  172 |   if (ctx.isProvisioning) {
  173 |     const code: GateCode = "PROVISIONING";
  174 |     const action = defaultActionFor(code);
  175 |     return {
  176 |       ok: false,
  177 |       uiMode: defaultUiModeFor(attempt.domain, code, attempt.verb, intent),
  178 |       cta: { action, label: "Try again" },
  179 |       reason: {
  180 |         code,
  181 |         action,
  182 |         message: defaultMessageFor(code, attempt.verb),
  183 |         domain: attempt.domain,
  184 |       },
  185 |     };
  186 |   }
  187 | 
  188 |   // 2) Caps (domain explicit). These can apply to anonymous users.
  189 |   if (attempt.domain === "playback" && ctx.playbackCapReached) {
  190 |     const code: GateCode = "PLAYBACK_CAP_REACHED";
  191 |     const action = defaultActionFor(code);
  192 |     return {
  193 |       ok: false,
  194 |       uiMode: defaultUiModeFor(attempt.domain, code, attempt.verb, intent),
  195 |       cta: { action, label: "Unlock playback" },
  196 |       reason: {
  197 |         code,
  198 |         action,
  199 |         message: defaultMessageFor(code, attempt.verb),
  200 |         domain: attempt.domain,
  201 |       },
  202 |     };
  203 |   }
  204 | 
  205 |   if (attempt.domain === "journal" && ctx.journalReadCapReached) {
  206 |     const code: GateCode = "JOURNAL_READ_CAP_REACHED";
  207 |     const action = defaultActionFor(code);
  208 |     return {
  209 |       ok: false,
  210 |       uiMode: defaultUiModeFor(attempt.domain, code, attempt.verb, intent),
  211 |       cta: { action, label: "Unlock journal" },
  212 |       reason: {
  213 |         code,
  214 |         action,
  215 |         message: defaultMessageFor(code, attempt.verb),
  216 |         domain: attempt.domain,
  217 |       },
  218 |     };
  219 |   }
  220 | 
  221 |   if (attempt.domain === "exegesis" && ctx.exegesisThreadReadCapReached) {
  222 |     const code: GateCode = "EXEGESIS_THREAD_READ_CAP_REACHED";
  223 |     const action = defaultActionFor(code);
  224 |     return {
  225 |       ok: false,
  226 |       uiMode: defaultUiModeFor(attempt.domain, code, attempt.verb, intent),
  227 |       cta: { action, label: "Sign in" },
  228 |       reason: {
  229 |         code,
  230 |         action,
  231 |         message: defaultMessageFor(code, attempt.verb),
  232 |         domain: attempt.domain,
  233 |       },
  234 |     };
  235 |   }
  236 | 
  237 |   if (ctx.readReceiptsCapReached) {
  238 |     const code: GateCode = "READ_RECEIPTS_CAP_REACHED";
  239 |     const action = defaultActionFor(code);
  240 |     return {
  241 |       ok: false,
  242 |       uiMode: defaultUiModeFor(attempt.domain, code, attempt.verb, intent),
  243 |       cta: { action, label: "Unlock identity" },
  244 |       reason: {
  245 |         code,
  246 |         action,
  247 |         message: defaultMessageFor(code, attempt.verb),
  248 |         domain: attempt.domain,
  249 |       },
  250 |     };
  251 |   }
  252 | 
  253 |   // 3) Auth gate (only for verbs that are not allowed anonymously).
  254 |   if (!ctx.isSignedIn && !anonAllowedVerb) {
  255 |     const code: GateCode = "AUTH_REQUIRED";
  256 |     const action = defaultActionFor(code);
  257 |     return {
  258 |       ok: false,
  259 |       uiMode: defaultUiModeFor(attempt.domain, code, attempt.verb, intent),
  260 |       cta: { action, label: "Sign in" },
  261 |       reason: {
  262 |         code,
  263 |         action,
  264 |         message: defaultMessageFor(code, attempt.verb),
  265 |         domain: attempt.domain,
  266 |       },
  267 |     };
  268 |   }
  269 | 
  270 |   // 4) Tier / entitlement gates (when provided by adapters).
  271 |   // These should generally be evaluated only when signed-in; otherwise you’ll prefer AUTH_REQUIRED.
  272 |   if (ctx.isSignedIn && ctx.hasTierAccess === false) {
  273 |     const code: GateCode = "TIER_REQUIRED";
  274 |     const action = defaultActionFor(code);
  275 |     return {
  276 |       ok: false,
  277 |       uiMode: defaultUiModeFor(attempt.domain, code, attempt.verb, intent),
  278 |       cta: { action, label: "Upgrade" },
  279 |       reason: {
  280 |         code,
  281 |         action,
  282 |         message: defaultMessageFor(code, attempt.verb),
  283 |         domain: attempt.domain,
  284 |       },
  285 |     };
  286 |   }
  287 | 
  288 |   if (ctx.isSignedIn && ctx.hasEntitlement === false) {
  289 |     const code: GateCode = "ENTITLEMENT_REQUIRED";
  290 |     const action = defaultActionFor(code);
  291 |     return {
  292 |       ok: false,
  293 |       uiMode: defaultUiModeFor(attempt.domain, code, attempt.verb, intent),
  294 |       cta: { action, label: "Become a member" },
  295 |       reason: {
  296 |         code,
  297 |         action,
  298 |         message: defaultMessageFor(code, attempt.verb),
  299 |         domain: attempt.domain,
  300 |       },
  301 |     };
  302 |   }
  303 | 
  304 |   return { ok: true };
  305 | }
  306 | 
  307 | /**
  308 |  * Helper for normalizing server-provided codes when you know the domain.
  309 |  * This is intentionally here (engine layer) so adapters use the same rules.
  310 |  */
  311 | export function normalizeServerGateCode(
  312 |   raw: GateCodeRaw,
  313 |   domain: GateDomain,
  314 | ): GateCodeRaw {
  315 |   return canonicalizeLegacyCapCode(raw, domain);
  316 | }

   30 |  * Temporary compatibility codes still emitted by legacy endpoints.

   32 |  */
   33 | export type LegacyGateCode = "ANON_CAP_REACHED" | "CAP_REACHED";
   34 | 
   35 | /**
   36 |  * “Raw” is what the outside world may hand us while migration is in progress.
   37 |  * Internal gate policy should use canonical GateCode where possible.
   38 |  */
   39 | export type GateCodeRaw = GateCode | LegacyGateCode;
   40 | 
   41 | export type GateReason = {
   42 |   code: GateCodeRaw;
   43 |   action: GateAction;
   44 |   message: string;
   45 |   correlationId?: string | null;
   46 |   domain: GateDomain;
   47 | };
   48 | 
   49 | /**
   50 |  * Normalize any raw/legacy string into a known code (canonical or legacy).
   51 |  * Unknown strings => null.
   52 |  */
   53 | export function normalizeGateCodeRaw(
   54 |   raw: string | null | undefined,
   55 | ): GateCodeRaw | null {
   56 |   const c = (raw ?? "").trim();
   57 |   if (!c) return null;
   58 | 
   59 |   switch (c) {
   60 |     // canonical
   61 |     case "AUTH_REQUIRED":
   62 |     case "ENTITLEMENT_REQUIRED":
   63 |     case "TIER_REQUIRED":
   64 |     case "EMBARGO":
   65 |     case "PROVISIONING":
   66 |     case "READ_RECEIPTS_CAP_REACHED":
   67 |     case "PLAYBACK_CAP_REACHED":
   68 |     case "JOURNAL_READ_CAP_REACHED":
   69 |     case "EXEGESIS_THREAD_READ_CAP_REACHED":
   70 |     case "INVALID_REQUEST":
   71 |       return c;
   72 | 
   73 |     // legacy
   74 |     case "ANON_CAP_REACHED":
   75 |     case "CAP_REACHED":
   76 |       return c;
   77 | 
   78 |     default:
   79 |       return null;
   80 |   }
   81 | }
   82 | 
   83 | /**
   84 |  * Helper for adapters that only have “raw string” codes.
   85 |  * Unknown strings => null.
   86 |  */
   87 | export function parseGateCodeRaw(
   88 |   raw: string | null | undefined,
   89 | ): GateCodeRaw | null {
   90 |   return normalizeGateCodeRaw(raw);
   91 | }
   92 | 
   93 | /**
   94 |  * One-way canonicalization helper for legacy cap codes.
   95 |  * Use at boundaries when you KNOW the domain context (e.g. playback vs receipts).
   96 |  */
   97 | export function canonicalizeLegacyCapCode(
   98 |   raw: GateCodeRaw,
   99 |   domain: GateDomain,
  100 | ): GateCodeRaw {
  101 |   if (raw !== "ANON_CAP_REACHED" && raw !== "CAP_REACHED") return raw;
  102 | 
  103 |   switch (domain) {
  104 |     case "playback":
  105 |       return "PLAYBACK_CAP_REACHED";
  106 |     case "journal":
  107 |       return "JOURNAL_READ_CAP_REACHED";
  108 |     case "exegesis":
  109 |       return "EXEGESIS_THREAD_READ_CAP_REACHED";
  110 |     default:
  111 |       // No silent semantic drift: if a legacy cap arrives without a known mapping,
  112 |       // keep it legacy and let the adapter/engine handle it explicitly.
  113 |       return raw;
  114 |   }
  115 | }
  116 | 
  117 | /**
  118 |  * Standard server->client blocked payload shape (future-proof).
  119 |  * (Not yet enforced repo-wide; introduced now so endpoints can converge.)
  120 |  */
  121 | export type GatePayload = {
  122 |   code: GateCodeRaw;
  123 |   action: GateAction;
  124 |   domain: GateDomain;
  125 |   message: string;
  126 |   correlationId?: string | null;
  127 | 
  128 |   /**
  129 |    * Optional extra detail for logs / UX copy variants.
  130 |    * Keep as string so the server doesn't leak UI policy.
  131 |    */
  132 |   reason?: string;
  133 | };

  134 | function loadTrackCached(tid: string): Promise<LyricsOk> {
  135 |   const key = tid.trim();
  136 |   if (!key) return Promise.reject(new Error("Missing recordingId"));
  137 | 
  138 |   const hit = TRACK_CACHE.get(key);
  139 |   if (hit) return Promise.resolve(hit);
  140 | 
  141 |   const inflight = TRACK_PROMISES.get(key);
  142 |   if (inflight) return inflight;
  143 | 
  144 |   const p = (async () => {
  145 |     const url = `/api/lyrics/by-track?recordingId=${encodeURIComponent(key)}`;
  146 |     const r = await fetch(url, { cache: "no-store" });
  147 |     const j = (await r.json()) as LyricsOk | LyricsErr;
  148 |     if (!j.ok) throw new Error(j.error || "Failed to load lyrics.");
  149 |     TRACK_CACHE.set(key, j);
  150 |     return j;
  151 |   })().finally(() => {
  152 |     TRACK_PROMISES.delete(key);
  153 |   });
  154 | 
  155 |   TRACK_PROMISES.set(key, p);
  156 |   return p;
  157 | }

  178 | // ---- cover tint cache (module scope) ----
  179 | const COVER_TINT_CACHE = new Map<string, string>(); // url -> "rgba(r,g,b,a)"
  180 | const COVER_TINT_INFLIGHT = new Map<string, Promise<string | null>>();
  181 | 
  182 | function clamp255(n: number) {
  183 |   return Math.max(0, Math.min(255, Math.round(n)));
  184 | }
  185 | 
  186 | function computeAverageRgb(data: Uint8ClampedArray) {
  187 |   let r = 0;
  188 |   let g = 0;
  189 |   let b = 0;
  190 |   let count = 0;
  191 | 
  192 |   for (let i = 0; i < data.length; i += 4) {
  193 |     const a = data[i + 3] ?? 0;
  194 |     if (a < 16) continue;
  195 | 
  196 |     const rr = data[i] ?? 0;
  197 |     const gg = data[i + 1] ?? 0;
  198 |     const bb = data[i + 2] ?? 0;
  199 | 
  200 |     if (rr + gg + bb < 36) continue;
  201 | 
  202 |     r += rr;
  203 |     g += gg;
  204 |     b += bb;
  205 |     count++;
  206 |   }
  207 | 
  208 |   if (!count) return null;
  209 | 
  210 |   r /= count;
  211 |   g /= count;
  212 |   b /= count;
  213 | 
  214 |   // lift slightly for visibility
  215 |   const base = {
  216 |     r: clamp255(r + 18),
  217 |     g: clamp255(g + 18),
  218 |     b: clamp255(b + 18),
  219 |   };
  220 | 
  221 |   // darker companion tone
  222 |   const dark = {
  223 |     r: clamp255(base.r * 0.65),
  224 |     g: clamp255(base.g * 0.65),
  225 |     b: clamp255(base.b * 0.65),
  226 |   };
  227 | 
  228 |   return { base, dark };
  229 | }
  230 | 
  231 | async function extractCoverTint(url: string): Promise<string | null> {
  232 |   const key = (url ?? "").trim();
  233 |   if (!key) return null;
  234 | 
  235 |   const cached = COVER_TINT_CACHE.get(key);
  236 |   if (cached) return cached;
  237 | 
  238 |   const inflight = COVER_TINT_INFLIGHT.get(key);
  239 |   if (inflight) return inflight;
  240 | 
  241 |   const p = (async () => {
  242 |     try {
  243 |       const img = new Image();
  244 |       img.crossOrigin = "anonymous";
  245 |       img.decoding = "async";
  246 | 
  247 |       const loaded = new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
  248 |         img.onload = () => resolve();
  249 |         img.onerror = () => reject(new Error("img_load_failed"));
  250 |       });
  251 | 
  252 |       img.src = key;
  253 |       await loaded;
  254 | 
  255 |       // downsample hard for speed
  256 |       const size = 36;
  257 |       const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
  258 |       canvas.width = size;
  259 |       canvas.height = size;
  260 | 
  261 |       const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d", { willReadFrequently: true });
  262 |       if (!ctx) return null;
  263 | 
  264 |       ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, size, size);
  265 |       const { data } = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, size, size);
  266 | 
  267 |       const avg = computeAverageRgb(data);
  268 |       if (!avg) return null;
  269 | 
  270 |       const base = `rgba(${avg.base.r}, ${avg.base.g}, ${avg.base.b}, 0.45)`;
  271 |       const dark = `rgba(${avg.dark.r}, ${avg.dark.g}, ${avg.dark.b}, 0.45)`;
  272 | 
  273 |       const gradient = `linear-gradient(135deg, ${base}, ${dark})`;
  274 |       COVER_TINT_CACHE.set(key, gradient);
  275 |       return gradient;
  276 |     } catch {
  277 |       return null;
  278 |     } finally {
  279 |       COVER_TINT_INFLIGHT.delete(key);
  280 |     }
  281 |   })();
  282 | 
  283 |   COVER_TINT_INFLIGHT.set(key, p);
  284 |   const out = await p;
  285 |   return out;
  286 | }

  448 | export default function PortalExegesis(props: { title?: string }) {

  543 |     void router.prefetch(target);

   31 |   const s = Math.max(0, Math.floor(ms / 1000));
   32 |   const m = Math.floor(s / 60);
   33 |   const r = s % 60;
   34 |   return `${m}:${String(r).padStart(2, "0")}`;

  202 |   "ANON_CAP_REACHED",
  203 |   "CAP_REACHED",
  204 | ] as const;

  206 | function isPlaybackGateCode(v: unknown): v is GateCodeRaw {
  207 |   return (
  208 |     typeof v === "string" &&
  209 |     (PLAYBACK_GATE_CODES as readonly string[]).includes(v)
  210 |   );
  211 | }
  212 | 
  213 | function toGateAction(v: unknown): GateAction {
  214 |   return isPlaybackAccessAction(v) ? v : "login";
  215 | }
  216 | 
  217 | function playbackGateMessage(reason: string | null | undefined): string {
  218 |   const trimmed = reason?.trim();
  219 |   return trimmed || "Enter your email address to continue listening.";
  220 | }

  222 | function formatShareTokenExpiry(iso: string | null): string | null {
  223 |   if (!iso) return null;
  224 | 
  225 |   const ms = Date.parse(iso);
  226 |   if (!Number.isFinite(ms)) return null;
  227 | 
  228 |   return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(undefined, {
  229 |     day: "numeric",
  230 |     month: "long",
  231 |     year: "numeric",
  232 |     hour: "numeric",
  233 |     minute: "2-digit",
  234 |   }).format(new Date(ms));
  235 | }
  236 | 
  237 | function formatRedemptionLimit(maxRedemptions: number | null): string | null {
  238 |   if (maxRedemptions == null) return null;
  239 | 
  240 |   return `The link will work ${maxRedemptions} time${
  241 |     maxRedemptions === 1 ? "" : "s"
  242 |   }.`;
  243 | }

  245 | type StableView = AlbumPlayerBundle;
  246 | 
  247 | type StreamingPlatform =
  248 |   | "spotify"
  249 |   | "appleMusic"
  250 |   | "youtubeMusic"
  251 |   | "amazonMusic"
  252 |   | "tidal"
  253 |   | "deezer";
  254 | 
  255 | type PlatformLink = { platform: StreamingPlatform; url: string };
  256 | 
  257 | function isStreamingPlatform(v: unknown): v is StreamingPlatform {
  258 |   return (
  259 |     v === "spotify" ||
  260 |     v === "appleMusic" ||
  261 |     v === "youtubeMusic" ||
  262 |     v === "amazonMusic" ||
  263 |     v === "tidal" ||
  264 |     v === "deezer"
  265 |   );
  266 | }
  267 | 
  268 | function isPlatformLink(v: unknown): v is PlatformLink {
  269 |   if (typeof v !== "object" || v === null) return false;
  270 | 
  271 |   const maybe = v as Record<string, unknown>;
  272 | 
  273 |   return (
  274 |     isStreamingPlatform(maybe.platform) &&
  275 |     typeof maybe.url === "string" &&
  276 |     /^https:\/\//i.test(maybe.url)
  277 |   );
  278 | }

  280 | function normalizePlatformLinks(
  281 |   links:
  282 |     | NonNullable<AlbumPlayerBundle["album"]>["platformLinks"]
  283 |     | null
  284 |     | undefined,
  285 | ): PlatformLink[] {
  286 |   if (!Array.isArray(links)) return [];
  287 |   return links.filter(isPlatformLink);
  288 | }

  374 | 
  375 | function AvailableOnRibbon({ links }: { links: PlatformLink[] }) {
  376 |   if (!links.length) return null;
  377 | 
  378 |   const order: StreamingPlatform[] = [
  379 |     "spotify",
  380 |     "appleMusic",
  381 |     "youtubeMusic",
  382 |     "amazonMusic",
  383 |     "tidal",
  384 |     "deezer",
  385 |   ];
  386 | 
  387 |   const byPlatform = new Map<StreamingPlatform, string>();
  388 |   for (const l of links) byPlatform.set(l.platform, l.url);
  389 | 
  390 |   const ordered = order
  391 |     .map((p) => ({ platform: p, url: byPlatform.get(p) }))
  392 |     .filter((x): x is { platform: StreamingPlatform; url: string } =>
  393 |       Boolean(x.url),
  394 |     );
  395 | 
  396 |   if (!ordered.length) return null;
  397 | 
  398 |   return (
  399 |     <div className="afAvailWrap" aria-label="Available on">
  400 |       <div className="afAvailLine" aria-hidden="true" />
  401 |       <div className="afAvailLabel">AVAILABLE ON</div>
  402 | 
  403 |       <div className="afAvailIcons">
  404 |         {ordered.map(({ platform, url }) => (
  405 |           <a
  406 |             key={platform}
  407 |             href={url}
  408 |             target="_blank"
  409 |             rel="noopener noreferrer external nofollow"
  410 |             className="afAvailIcon"
  411 |             aria-label={`Open on ${platform}`}
  412 |             title={`Open on ${platform}`}
  413 |           >
  414 |             <PlatformIcon platform={platform} />
  415 |           </a>
  416 |         ))}
  417 |       </div>
  418 |     </div>
  419 |   );
  420 | }
  421 | 
  422 | function parsePublicAlbumPath(pathname: string | null): {
  423 |   albumSlug: string | null;
  424 |   displayId: string | null;
  425 | } {
  426 |   const p = (pathname ?? "").trim();
  427 | 
  428 |   // canonical music surfaces:
  429 |   // /:slug
  430 |   // /:slug/:displayId
  431 |   //
  432 |   // exclude obvious non-music reserved roots
  433 |   const reserved = new Set([
  434 |     "",
  435 |     "player",
  436 |     "journal",
  437 |     "portal",
  438 |     "posts",
  439 |     "extras",
  440 |     "download",
  441 |     "gift",
  442 |     "unsubscribe",
  443 |     "studio",
  444 |     "admin",
  445 |     "api",
  446 |   ]);
  447 | 
  448 |   const parts = p
  449 |     .split("?")[0]
  450 |     .split("#")[0]
  451 |     .split("/")
  452 |     .filter(Boolean)
  453 |     .map((x) => decodeURIComponent(x).trim())
  454 |     .filter(Boolean);
  455 | 
  456 |   if (parts.length === 0) return { albumSlug: null, displayId: null };
  457 |   if (parts.length > 2) return { albumSlug: null, displayId: null };
  458 | 
  459 |   const albumSlug = parts[0] ?? null;
  460 |   if (!albumSlug || reserved.has(albumSlug)) {
  461 |     return { albumSlug: null, displayId: null };
  462 |   }
  463 | 
  464 |   const displayId = parts[1] ?? null;
  465 |   return { albumSlug, displayId };
  466 | }
  467 | 
  468 | function canonicalCarryQuery(): string {
  469 |   // Only carry the params that are allowed to exist on canonical public routes.
  470 |   if (typeof window === "undefined") return "";
  471 |   const sp = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
  472 |   const out = new URLSearchParams();
  473 | 
  474 |   const st = (sp.get("st") ?? "").trim();
  475 |   const share = (sp.get("share") ?? "").trim();
  476 |   const autoplay = (sp.get("autoplay") ?? "").trim();
  477 | 
  478 |   if (st) out.set("st", st);
  479 |   else if (share) out.set("share", share);
  480 | 
  481 |   if (autoplay) out.set("autoplay", autoplay);
  482 | 
  483 |   // keep utm_* if present
  484 |   for (const [k, v] of sp.entries()) {
  485 |     if (k.startsWith("utm_") && v.trim()) out.set(k, v.trim());
  486 |   }
  487 | 
  488 |   const q = out.toString();
  489 |   return q ? `?${q}` : "";
  490 | }
  491 | 
  492 | export default function FullPlayer(props: {
  493 |   bundle: AlbumPlayerBundle;
  494 |   albums: AlbumNavItem[];
  495 |   onSelectAlbum?: (slug: string) => void;
  496 |   isBrowsingAlbum?: boolean;
  497 |   tier?: Tier;
  498 | }) {
  499 |   const {
  500 |     bundle,
  501 |     albums,
  502 |     onSelectAlbum,
  503 |     isBrowsingAlbum = false,
  504 |     tier = "none",
  505 |   } = props;
  506 | 
  507 |   const { albumSlug, album, tracks, albumLyrics } = bundle;
  508 |   const p = usePlayer();
  509 |   const { reportGate } = useGateBroker();
  510 |   const {
  511 |     isLoaded: clerkAuthLoaded,
  512 |     isSignedIn,
  513 |     userId,
  514 |     sessionId,
  515 |   } = useAuth();
  516 | 
  517 |   const accessIdentityKey = !clerkAuthLoaded
  518 |     ? "clerk:loading"
  519 |     : isSignedIn
  520 |       ? `clerk:user:${userId ?? ""}:session:${sessionId ?? ""}`
  521 |       : "clerk:anonymous";
  522 | 
  523 |   // near your other hooks
  524 |   const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  525 |   const stParam =
  526 |     (searchParams?.get("st") ?? searchParams?.get("share") ?? "").trim() ||
  527 |     null;
  528 | 
  529 |   const pRef = React.useRef(p);
  530 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  531 |     pRef.current = p;
  532 |   }, [p]);
  533 | 
  534 |   // ---- optimistic UI cache (state, not ref) to satisfy react-hooks/refs lint ----
  535 |   const [stableView, setStableView] = React.useState<StableView | null>(null);
  536 | 
  537 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  538 |     if (album && tracks && tracks.length) {
  539 |       setStableView(bundle);
  540 |     }
  541 |     primeLyricsFromAlbumBundle(albumLyrics ?? null);
  542 |   }, [bundle, album, tracks, albumLyrics]);
  543 | 
  544 |   const showCached = Boolean(
  545 |     isBrowsingAlbum && stableView?.album && (!album || !tracks?.length),
  546 |   );
  547 |   const effAlbumSlug = showCached ? stableView!.albumSlug : albumSlug;
  548 |   const effAlbum = showCached ? stableView!.album : album;
  549 |   const effTracks = showCached ? stableView!.tracks : tracks;
  550 |   // Track membership ref (lets access-check logic test membership without re-triggering fetch)
  551 |   const effRecordingIdSetRef = React.useRef<Set<string>>(new Set());
  552 | 
  553 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  554 |     effRecordingIdSetRef.current = new Set(effTracks.map((t) => t.recordingId));
  555 |   }, [effTracks]);
  556 | 
  557 |   const router = useRouter();
  558 |   const pathname = usePathname();
  559 | 
  560 |   const route = React.useMemo(() => parsePublicAlbumPath(pathname), [pathname]);
  561 |   const isPublicAlbumRoute = Boolean(route.albumSlug);
  562 | 
  563 |   const goCanonicalTrack = React.useCallback(
  564 |     (displayId: string | null, mode: "push" | "replace" = "push") => {
  565 |       if (!isPublicAlbumRoute) return;
  566 | 
  567 |       const qs = canonicalCarryQuery();
  568 |       const base = `/${encodeURIComponent(effAlbumSlug)}`;
  569 |       const href = displayId
  570 |         ? `${base}/${encodeURIComponent(displayId)}${qs}`
  571 |         : `${base}${qs}`;
  572 | 
  573 |       if (mode === "replace") router.replace(href, { scroll: false });
  574 |       else router.push(href, { scroll: false });
  575 |     },
  576 |     [isPublicAlbumRoute, router, effAlbumSlug],
  577 |   );
  578 | 
  579 |   const [pendingAlbumSlug, setPendingAlbumSlug] = React.useState<string | null>(
  580 |     null,
  581 |   );
  582 |   const fullPlayerTopRef = React.useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  583 |   const mobileScrollTargetAlbumSlugRef = React.useRef<string | null>(null);
  584 | 
  585 |   const scrollMobilePlayerToTop = React.useCallback(() => {
  586 |     if (typeof window === "undefined") return;
  587 |     if (!window.matchMedia("(max-width: 640px)").matches) return;
  588 | 
  589 |     const target = fullPlayerTopRef.current;
  590 |     if (!target) return;
  591 | 
  592 |     let ancestor: HTMLElement | null = target.parentElement;
  593 | 
  594 |     while (ancestor) {
  595 |       const overflowY = window.getComputedStyle(ancestor).overflowY;
  596 |       const canScroll =
  597 |         (overflowY === "auto" ||
  598 |           overflowY === "scroll" ||
  599 |           overflowY === "overlay") &&
  600 |         ancestor.scrollHeight > ancestor.clientHeight;
  601 | 
  602 |       if (canScroll) {

  611 |           top: nextTop,
  612 |           behavior: "auto",
  613 |         });
  614 | 
  615 |         return;
  616 |       }
  617 | 
  618 |       ancestor = ancestor.parentElement;
  619 |     }

  621 |     const targetTop = window.scrollY + target.getBoundingClientRect().top;

  623 |     window.scrollTo({

  734 |     async function refreshActiveListeners(): Promise<void> {

  826 |           if (prev && JSON.stringify(prev) === JSON.stringify(next))
  827 |             return prev;

  831 |         const player = pRef.current;

  904 |         // PlayerState must still fail closed if real play authorisation fails.
  905 |         setAccess(fallback);

  907 |         const player = pRef.current;
  908 |         if (player.lastError) player.clearError();
  909 |       }
  910 |     })();

  913 |       alive = false;
  914 |     };

 1149 |   function ensureAlbumQueue() {
 1150 |     // Explicit nulls prevent an earlier private-link attribution context from
 1151 |     // surviving when ordinary album playback replaces the queue.
 1152 |     p.setQueue(effTracks, {
 1153 |       contextId: albumKey ?? undefined,
 1154 |       artworkUrl: effAlbum?.artworkUrl ?? null,
 1155 |       contextSlug: effAlbumSlug,
 1156 |       contextTitle: effAlbum?.title ?? undefined,
 1157 |       contextArtist: effAlbum?.artist ?? undefined,
 1158 |       sharePlaybackContext: queueSharePlaybackContext,
 1159 |       sharePlaybackScopeId: queueSharePlaybackScopeId,
 1160 |     });
 1161 |   }
 1162 | 
 1163 |   const preparedInitialQueueRef = React.useRef<string | null>(null);
 1164 | 
 1165 |   React.useEffect(() => {
 1166 |     // Wait for the access check so a private-link queue receives its resolved
 1167 |     // attribution context before any listener can press play.
 1168 |     if (!accessKnown) return;
 1169 | 
 1170 |     if (!isPublicAlbumRoute || route.albumSlug !== effAlbumSlug || !albumKey) {
 1171 |       return;
 1172 |     }
 1173 | 
 1174 |     const directTrack = route.displayId
 1175 |       ? (effTracks.find((track) => track.displayId === route.displayId) ?? null)
 1176 |       : null;
 1177 | 
 1178 |     const initialTrack = directTrack ?? effTracks[0] ?? null;
 1179 | 
 1180 |     if (!initialTrack) return;
 1181 | 
 1182 |     // Prepare a fresh player around the route's target track—or track one on a
 1183 |     // generic album route—without beginning playback. Never overwrite an
 1184 |     // existing session, including a paused or playing different album.
 1185 |     const hasExistingPlayerSession = Boolean(
 1186 |       p.current || p.queue.length > 0 || p.pendingRecordingId,
 1187 |     );
 1188 | 
 1189 |     if (hasExistingPlayerSession) return;
 1190 | 
 1191 |     const queueKey = `${albumKey}:${initialTrack.recordingId}`;
 1192 | 
 1193 |     if (preparedInitialQueueRef.current === queueKey) return;
 1194 | 
 1195 |     preparedInitialQueueRef.current = queueKey;
 1196 | 
 1197 |     p.setQueue(effTracks, {
 1198 |       contextId: albumKey,
 1199 |       artworkUrl: effAlbum?.artworkUrl ?? null,
 1200 |       contextSlug: effAlbumSlug,
 1201 |       contextTitle: effAlbum?.title ?? undefined,
 1202 |       contextArtist: effAlbum?.artist ?? undefined,
 1203 |       sharePlaybackContext: queueSharePlaybackContext,
 1204 |       sharePlaybackScopeId: queueSharePlaybackScopeId,
 1205 |       initialCurrentRecordingId: initialTrack.recordingId,
 1206 |     });
 1207 |   }, [
 1208 |     accessKnown,
 1209 |     albumKey,
 1210 |     effAlbum?.artworkUrl,
 1211 |     effAlbum?.artist,
 1212 |     effAlbum?.title,
 1213 |     effAlbumSlug,
 1214 |     effTracks,
 1215 |     isPublicAlbumRoute,
 1216 |     p,
 1217 |     queueSharePlaybackContext,
 1218 |     queueSharePlaybackScopeId,
 1219 |     route.albumSlug,
 1220 |     route.displayId,
 1221 |   ]);

 1223 |   function playAlbumIndex(i: number) {
 1224 |     const t = effTracks[i];
 1225 |     if (!t) return;
 1226 | 
 1227 |     prefetchAlbumSession(t);
 1228 |     ensureAlbumQueue();
 1229 |     if (isPublicAlbumRoute) {
 1230 |       // User-initiated navigation; preserve history.
 1231 |       goCanonicalTrack(t.displayId, "push");
 1232 |     }
 1233 | 
 1234 |     p.play(t);
 1235 |     window.dispatchEvent(new Event("af:play-intent"));
 1236 |   }

  130 | const PlayerVisualCtx = React.createContext<PlayerVisualState | null>(null);
