  132 | function clamp(n: number, lo: number, hi: number) {
  133 |   return Math.max(lo, Math.min(hi, n));
  134 | }
  135 | 
  136 | function nextRepeat(r: RepeatMode): RepeatMode {
  137 |   if (r === "off") return "all";
  138 |   if (r === "all") return "one";
  139 |   return "off";
  140 | }
  141 | 
  142 | function positiveDurationMs(value: number | undefined): number {
  143 |   return typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value) && value > 0
  144 |     ? Math.round(value)
  145 |     : 0;
  146 | }
  147 | 
  148 | function getAssetDurationMs(
  149 |   assetDurationByPlaybackId: Record<string, number>,
  150 |   track: PlayerTrack | undefined,
  151 | ): number {
  152 |   const playbackId = (track?.muxPlaybackId ?? "").trim();
  153 | 
  154 |   return playbackId
  155 |     ? positiveDurationMs(assetDurationByPlaybackId[playbackId])
  156 |     : 0;
  157 | }
  158 | 
  159 | function getEffectiveDurationMs(
  160 |   state: Pick<
  161 |     PlayerState,
  162 |     "durationByRecordingId" | "assetDurationByPlaybackId"
  163 |   >,
  164 |   track: PlayerTrack | undefined,
  165 | ): number {
  166 |   const assetDurationMs = getAssetDurationMs(
  167 |     state.assetDurationByPlaybackId,
  168 |     track,
  169 |   );
  170 | 
  171 |   if (assetDurationMs > 0) return assetDurationMs;
  172 | 
  173 |   const recordingId = (track?.recordingId ?? "").trim();
  174 |   const catalogueDurationMs = recordingId
  175 |     ? positiveDurationMs(state.durationByRecordingId[recordingId])
  176 |     : 0;
  177 | 
  178 |   return catalogueDurationMs || positiveDurationMs(track?.durationMs);
  179 | }
  180 | 
  181 | function retainAssetDurationsByPlaybackId(
  182 |   previous: Record<string, number>,
  183 |   tracks: PlayerTrack[],
  184 | ): Record<string, number> {
  185 |   const activePlaybackIds = new Set<string>();
  186 | 
  187 |   for (const track of tracks) {
  188 |     const playbackId = (track.muxPlaybackId ?? "").trim();
  189 |     if (playbackId) activePlaybackIds.add(playbackId);
  190 |   }
  191 | 
  192 |   let next = previous;
  193 | 
  194 |   for (const playbackId of Object.keys(previous)) {
  195 |     if (activePlaybackIds.has(playbackId)) continue;
  196 | 
  197 |     if (next === previous) next = { ...previous };
  198 |     delete next[playbackId];
  199 |   }
  200 | 
  201 |   return next;
  202 | }
  203 | 
  204 | function hydrateTrack(
  205 |   t: PlayerTrack,
  206 |   durationByRecordingId: Record<string, number>,
  207 | ): PlayerTrack {
  208 |   const cached = durationByRecordingId[t.recordingId];
  209 |   if (!cached || cached <= 0) return t;
  210 |   if (t.durationMs === cached) return t;
  211 |   return { ...t, durationMs: cached };
  212 | }
  213 | 
  214 | function hydrateTracks(
  215 |   ts: PlayerTrack[],
  216 |   durationByRecordingId: Record<string, number>,
  217 | ) {
  218 |   let changed = false;
  219 |   const next = ts.map((t) => {
  220 |     const ht = hydrateTrack(t, durationByRecordingId);
  221 |     if (ht !== t) changed = true;
  222 |     return ht;
  223 |   });
  224 |   return changed ? next : ts;
  225 | }
  226 | 
  227 | function blockedPlaybackMessage(access: PlaybackAccessDecision): string {
  228 |   if (access.embargoed) {
  229 |     return "Playback is disabled pre-release.";
  230 |   }
  231 | 
  232 |   if (access.reason) return access.reason;
  233 | 
  234 |   return "Playback is not available for this release.";
  235 | }
  236 | 
  237 | type TargetedPlayIntentDetail = {
  238 |   track: PlayerTrack;
  239 |   albumId: string | null;
  240 | };
  241 | 
  242 | function dispatchTargetedPlayIntent(
  243 |   track: PlayerTrack,
  244 |   state: Pick<PlayerState, "queueContextId">,
  245 | ): void {
  246 |   if (typeof window === "undefined") return;
  247 | 
  248 |   const albumId = (state.queueContextId ?? "").trim() || null;
  249 | 
  250 |   window.dispatchEvent(
  251 |     new CustomEvent<TargetedPlayIntentDetail>("af:play-target-intent", {
  252 |       detail: {
  253 |         track,
  254 |         albumId,
  255 |       },
  256 |     }),
  257 |   );
  258 | }
  259 | 
  260 | function getNextTransportTrack(state: PlayerState): PlayerTrack | undefined {
  261 |   const current = state.current;
  262 | 
  263 |   if (!current || state.queue.length === 0) return undefined;
  264 |   if (state.repeat === "one") return current;
  265 | 
  266 |   const index = state.queue.findIndex(
  267 |     (track) => track.recordingId === current.recordingId,
  268 |   );
  269 |   const currentIndex = index >= 0 ? index : 0;
  270 | 
  271 |   if (currentIndex + 1 < state.queue.length) {
  272 |     return state.queue[currentIndex + 1];
  273 |   }
  274 | 
  275 |   return state.repeat === "all" ? state.queue[0] : undefined;
  276 | }
  277 | 
  278 | function getPreviousTransportTrack(
  279 |   state: PlayerState,
  280 | ): PlayerTrack | undefined {
  281 |   const current = state.current;
  282 | 
  283 |   if (!current || state.queue.length === 0) return undefined;
  284 | 
  285 |   if (state.status === "playing" && state.positionMs > 3000) {
  286 |     return current;
  287 |   }
  288 | 
  289 |   const index = state.queue.findIndex(
  290 |     (track) => track.recordingId === current.recordingId,
  291 |   );
  292 |   const currentIndex = index >= 0 ? index : 0;
  293 | 
  294 |   if (currentIndex > 0) {
  295 |     return state.queue[currentIndex - 1];
  296 |   }
  297 | 
  298 |   if (state.repeat === "all") {
  299 |     return state.queue[state.queue.length - 1];
  300 |   }
  301 | 
  302 |   return current;
  303 | }
  304 | 
  305 | function primeDurationByRecordingId(
  306 |   prev: Record<string, number>,
  307 |   tracks: PlayerTrack[],
  308 | ): Record<string, number> {
  309 |   let next = prev;
  310 |   for (const t of tracks) {
  311 |     if (!t?.recordingId) continue;
  312 |     const ms = t.durationMs;
  313 |     if (typeof ms !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(ms) || ms <= 0) continue;
  314 |     if (typeof next[t.recordingId] === "number" && next[t.recordingId] > 0)
  315 |       continue;
  316 |     if (next === prev) next = { ...prev };
  317 |     next[t.recordingId] = ms;
  318 |   }
  319 |   return next;
  320 | }
  321 | 
  322 | export function PlayerStateProvider(props: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  323 |   const {
  324 |     isLoaded: clerkAuthLoaded,
  325 |     isSignedIn,
  326 |     userId,
  327 |     sessionId,
  328 |   } = useAuth();
  329 | 
  330 |   const playbackAccessIdentityKey = !clerkAuthLoaded
  331 |     ? "clerk:loading"
  332 |     : isSignedIn
  333 |       ? `clerk:user:${userId ?? ""}:session:${sessionId ?? ""}`
  334 |       : "clerk:anonymous";
  335 | 
  336 |   const [state, setState] = React.useState<PlayerState>({
  337 |     status: "idle",
  338 |     current: undefined,
  339 |     queue: [],
  340 |     lastError: undefined,
  341 |     lastPlayAttemptAtMs: undefined,
  342 |     playRequestPending: false,
  343 | 
  344 |     queueContextId: undefined,
  345 |     queueContextSlug: undefined,
  346 |     queueContextTitle: undefined,
  347 |     queueContextArtist: undefined,
  348 |     queueContextArtworkUrl: null,
  349 |     queueSharePlaybackContext: null,
  350 |     queueSharePlaybackScopeId: null,
  351 | 
  352 |     intent: null,
  353 |     intentAtMs: undefined,
  354 |     selectedRecordingId: undefined,
  355 |     pendingRecordingId: undefined,
  356 | 
  357 |     pendingSeekMs: undefined,
  358 |     seeking: false,
  359 | 
  360 |     loadingReason: undefined,
  361 |     reloadNonce: 0,
  362 | 
  363 |     positionMs: 0,
  364 |     seekNonce: 0,
  365 | 
  366 |     volume: 0.9,
  367 |     muted: false,
  368 |     repeat: "off",
  369 | 
  370 |     durationByRecordingId: {},
  371 |     assetDurationByPlaybackId: {},
  372 |   });
  373 | 
  374 |   const stateRef = React.useRef(state);
  375 |   const playRequestSequenceRef = React.useRef(0);
  376 | 
  377 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  378 |     stateRef.current = state;
  379 |   }, [state]);
  380 | 
  381 |   const blockPlayback = React.useCallback((message: string) => {
  382 |     setState((s) => ({
  383 |       ...s,
  384 |       status: s.status === "loading" ? "idle" : s.status,
  385 |       intent: "pause",
  386 |       intentAtMs: Date.now(),
  387 |       playRequestPending: false,
  388 |       loadingReason: undefined,
  389 |       pendingRecordingId: undefined,
  390 |       lastError: message,
  391 |     }));
  392 |   }, []);
  393 | 
  394 |   const guardPlayback = React.useCallback(
  395 |     async (onAllowed: () => void): Promise<void> => {
  396 |       const catalogueId = (stateRef.current.queueContextId ?? "").trim();
  397 | 
  398 |       if (!catalogueId) {
  399 |         onAllowed();
  400 |         return;
  401 |       }
  402 | 
  403 |       try {
  404 |         const access = await fetchPlaybackAccessDecision({
  405 |           catalogueId,
  406 |           shareToken: readPlaybackShareTokenFromLocation(),
  407 |           accessIdentityKey: playbackAccessIdentityKey,
  408 |         });
  409 | 
  410 |         if (!access.allowed) {
  411 |           blockPlayback(blockedPlaybackMessage(access));
  412 |           return;
  413 |         }
  414 | 
  415 |         const expectedSharePlaybackScopeId = `alb:${catalogueId.replace(
  416 |           /^alb:/i,
  417 |           "",
  418 |         )}`;
  419 | 
  420 |         if (
  421 |           access.sharePlaybackContext &&
  422 |           access.sharePlaybackScopeId === expectedSharePlaybackScopeId
  423 |         ) {
  424 |           setState((current) => ({
  425 |             ...current,
  426 |             queueSharePlaybackContext: access.sharePlaybackContext,
  427 |             queueSharePlaybackScopeId: access.sharePlaybackScopeId,
  428 |           }));
  429 |         }
  430 | 
  431 |         onAllowed();
  432 |       } catch {
  433 |         blockPlayback("Playback access could not be verified.");
  434 |       }
  435 |     },
  436 |     [blockPlayback, playbackAccessIdentityKey],
  437 |   );
  438 |   const actions: PlayerActions = React.useMemo(() => {
  439 |     return {
  440 |       setIntent: (i: Intent) =>
  441 |         setState((s) => ({
  442 |           ...s,
  443 |           intent: i,
  444 |           intentAtMs: i ? Date.now() : undefined,
  445 |         })),
  446 | 
  447 |       clearIntent: () =>
  448 |         setState((s) =>
  449 |           s.intent ? { ...s, intent: null, intentAtMs: undefined } : s,
  450 |         ),
  451 | 
  452 |       selectTrack: (id?: string) =>
  453 |         setState((s) => {
  454 |           if (s.selectedRecordingId === id) return s;
  455 |           return {
  456 |             ...s,
  457 |             selectedRecordingId: id,
  458 |           };
  459 |         }),
  460 | 
  461 |       setPendingRecordingId: (id?: string) =>
  462 |         setState((s) => {
  463 |           if (s.pendingRecordingId === id) return s;
  464 |           return {
  465 |             ...s,
  466 |             pendingRecordingId: id,
  467 |           };
  468 |         }),
  469 | 
  470 |       resolvePendingTrack: (id: string) =>
  471 |         setState((s) => {
  472 |           if (s.pendingRecordingId !== id) return s;
  473 |           return { ...s, pendingRecordingId: undefined };
  474 |         }),
  475 | 
  476 |       play: (track?: PlayerTrack) => {
  477 |         const requestSequence = ++playRequestSequenceRef.current;
  478 |         const requestStartedAtMs = Date.now();
  479 |         const snapshot = stateRef.current;
  480 |         const requestedTrack = track ?? snapshot.current ?? snapshot.queue[0];
  481 | 
  482 |         if (requestedTrack) {
  483 |           dispatchTargetedPlayIntent(
  484 |             hydrateTrack(requestedTrack, snapshot.durationByRecordingId),
  485 |             snapshot,
  486 |           );
  487 |         }
  488 | 
  489 |         setState((s) => ({
  490 |           ...s,
  491 |           playRequestPending: true,
  492 |           lastPlayAttemptAtMs: requestStartedAtMs,
  493 |           lastError: undefined,
  494 |         }));
  495 | 
  496 |         void guardPlayback(() => {
  497 |           if (requestSequence !== playRequestSequenceRef.current) {
  498 |             return;
  499 |           }
  500 | 
  501 |           setState((s) => {
  502 |             if (requestSequence !== playRequestSequenceRef.current) {
  503 |               return s;
  504 |             }
  505 | 
  506 |             const now = Date.now();
  507 |             const rawNext = track ?? s.current ?? s.queue[0];
  508 | 
  509 |             if (!rawNext) {
  510 |               return {
  511 |                 ...s,
  512 |                 status: "idle",
  513 |                 current: undefined,
  514 |                 positionMs: 0,
  515 |                 intent: "play",
  516 |                 intentAtMs: now,
  517 |                 lastPlayAttemptAtMs: now,
  518 |                 playRequestPending: false,
  519 |                 lastError: undefined,
  520 |                 loadingReason: undefined,
  521 |                 pendingRecordingId: undefined,
  522 |               };
  523 |             }
  524 | 
  525 |             const nextTrack = hydrateTrack(rawNext, s.durationByRecordingId);
  526 |             const sameTrack = Boolean(
  527 |               s.current && s.current.recordingId === nextTrack.recordingId,
  528 |             );
  529 | 
  530 |             const base = {
  531 |               ...s,
  532 |               intent: "play" as const,
  533 |               intentAtMs: now,
  534 |               lastPlayAttemptAtMs: now,
  535 |               playRequestPending: false,
  536 |               lastError: undefined,
  537 |               selectedRecordingId: nextTrack.recordingId,
  538 |               pendingRecordingId: nextTrack.recordingId,
  539 |             };
  540 | 
  541 |             if (sameTrack && s.status === "paused") {
  542 |               return {
  543 |                 ...base,
  544 |                 current: hydrateTrack(s.current!, s.durationByRecordingId),
  545 |                 status: "paused",
  546 |                 loadingReason: undefined,
  547 |               };
  548 |             }
  549 | 
  550 |             if (
  551 |               sameTrack &&
  552 |               (s.status === "playing" || s.status === "loading")
  553 |             ) {
  554 |               return { ...base, loadingReason: s.loadingReason };
  555 |             }
  556 | 
  557 |             return {
  558 |               ...base,
  559 |               current: nextTrack,
  560 |               status: "loading",
  561 |               loadingReason: "token",
  562 |               positionMs: 0,
  563 |             };
  564 |           });
  565 |         });
  566 |       },
  567 | 
  568 |       pause: () => {
  569 |         playRequestSequenceRef.current += 1;
  570 | 
  571 |         setState((s) => ({
  572 |           ...s,
  573 |           intent: "pause",
  574 |           intentAtMs: Date.now(),
  575 |           playRequestPending: false,
  576 |         }));
  577 |       },
  578 | 
  579 |       next: () => {
  580 |         const snapshot = stateRef.current;
  581 |         const targetTrack = getNextTransportTrack(snapshot);
  582 | 
  583 |         if (targetTrack) {
  584 |           dispatchTargetedPlayIntent(
  585 |             hydrateTrack(targetTrack, snapshot.durationByRecordingId),
  586 |             snapshot,
  587 |           );
  588 |         }
  589 | 
  590 |         void guardPlayback(() => {
  591 |           setState((s) => {
  592 |             const cur = s.current;
  593 |             if (!cur || s.queue.length === 0) return s;
  594 | 
  595 |             const idx = s.queue.findIndex(
  596 |               (t) => t.recordingId === cur.recordingId,
  597 |             );
  598 |             const at = idx >= 0 ? idx : 0;
  599 | 
  600 |             if (s.repeat === "one") {
  601 |               return {
  602 |                 ...s,
  603 |                 status: "loading",
  604 |                 loadingReason: "attach",
  605 |                 positionMs: 0,
  606 |                 intent: "play",
  607 |                 intentAtMs: Date.now(),
  608 |                 pendingRecordingId: cur.recordingId,
  609 |                 selectedRecordingId: cur.recordingId,
  610 |               };
  611 |             }
  612 | 
  613 |             const nextIdx = at + 1;
  614 |             if (nextIdx < s.queue.length) {
  615 |               const t = hydrateTrack(s.queue[nextIdx], s.durationByRecordingId);
  616 |               return {
  617 |                 ...s,
  618 |                 current: t,
  619 |                 status: "loading",
  620 |                 loadingReason: "token",
  621 |                 positionMs: 0,
  622 |                 intent: "play",
  623 |                 intentAtMs: Date.now(),
  624 |                 pendingRecordingId: t.recordingId,
  625 |                 selectedRecordingId: t.recordingId,
  626 |               };
  627 |             }
  628 | 
  629 |             if (s.repeat === "all" && s.queue.length > 0) {
  630 |               const t = hydrateTrack(s.queue[0], s.durationByRecordingId);
  631 |               return {
  632 |                 ...s,
  633 |                 current: t,
  634 |                 status: "loading",
  635 |                 loadingReason: "token",
  636 |                 positionMs: 0,
  637 |                 intent: "play",
  638 |                 intentAtMs: Date.now(),
  639 |                 pendingRecordingId: t.recordingId,
  640 |                 selectedRecordingId: t.recordingId,
  641 |               };
  642 |             }
  643 | 
  644 |             return {
  645 |               ...s,
  646 |               status: "paused",
  647 |               intent: "pause",
  648 |               intentAtMs: Date.now(),
  649 |               positionMs: 0,
  650 |               pendingSeekMs: 0,
  651 |               seeking: true,
  652 |               seekNonce: s.seekNonce + 1,
  653 |               loadingReason: undefined,
  654 |               pendingRecordingId: undefined,
  655 |             };
  656 |           });
  657 |         });
  658 |       },
  659 | 
  660 |       advanceFromEngine: () => {
  661 |         setState((s) => {
  662 |           const cur = s.current;
  663 |           if (!cur || s.queue.length === 0) return s;
  664 | 
  665 |           const idx = s.queue.findIndex(
  666 |             (t) => t.recordingId === cur.recordingId,
  667 |           );
  668 |           const at = idx >= 0 ? idx : 0;
  669 | 
  670 |           if (s.repeat === "one") {
  671 |             return {
  672 |               ...s,
  673 |               status: "loading",
  674 |               loadingReason: "attach",
  675 |               positionMs: 0,
  676 |               intent: "play",
  677 |               intentAtMs: Date.now(),
  678 |               pendingRecordingId: cur.recordingId,
  679 |               selectedRecordingId: cur.recordingId,
  680 |             };
  681 |           }
  682 | 
  683 |           const nextIdx = at + 1;
  684 | 
  685 |           if (nextIdx < s.queue.length) {
  686 |             const t = hydrateTrack(s.queue[nextIdx], s.durationByRecordingId);
  687 |             return {
  688 |               ...s,
  689 |               current: t,
  690 |               status: "loading",
  691 |               loadingReason: "attach",
  692 |               positionMs: 0,
  693 |               intent: "play",
  694 |               intentAtMs: Date.now(),
  695 |               pendingRecordingId: t.recordingId,
  696 |               selectedRecordingId: t.recordingId,
  697 |             };
  698 |           }
  699 | 
  700 |           if (s.repeat === "all" && s.queue.length > 0) {
  701 |             const t = hydrateTrack(s.queue[0], s.durationByRecordingId);
  702 |             return {
  703 |               ...s,
  704 |               current: t,
  705 |               status: "loading",
  706 |               loadingReason: "attach",
  707 |               positionMs: 0,
  708 |               intent: "play",
  709 |               intentAtMs: Date.now(),
  710 |               pendingRecordingId: t.recordingId,
  711 |               selectedRecordingId: t.recordingId,
  712 |             };
  713 |           }
  714 | 
  715 |           return {
  716 |             ...s,
  717 |             status: "paused",
  718 |             intent: null,
  719 |             intentAtMs: undefined,
  720 |             positionMs: 0,
  721 |             pendingSeekMs: 0,
  722 |             seeking: true,
  723 |             seekNonce: s.seekNonce + 1,
  724 |             loadingReason: undefined,
  725 |             pendingRecordingId: undefined,
  726 |           };
  727 |         });
  728 |       },
  729 | 
  730 |       prev: () => {
  731 |         const snapshot = stateRef.current;
  732 |         const targetTrack = getPreviousTransportTrack(snapshot);
  733 | 
  734 |         if (targetTrack) {
  735 |           dispatchTargetedPlayIntent(
  736 |             hydrateTrack(targetTrack, snapshot.durationByRecordingId),
  737 |             snapshot,
  738 |           );
  739 |         }
  740 | 
  741 |         void guardPlayback(() => {
  742 |           setState((s) => {
  743 |             const cur = s.current;
  744 |             if (!cur || s.queue.length === 0) return s;
  745 | 
  746 |             if (s.status === "playing" && s.positionMs > 3000) {
  747 |               return {
  748 |                 ...s,
  749 |                 positionMs: 0,
  750 |                 status: "loading",
  751 |                 loadingReason: "attach",
  752 |                 intent: "play",
  753 |                 intentAtMs: Date.now(),
  754 |                 pendingRecordingId: cur.recordingId,
  755 |                 selectedRecordingId: cur.recordingId,
  756 |               };
  757 |             }
  758 | 
  759 |             const idx = s.queue.findIndex(
  760 |               (t) => t.recordingId === cur.recordingId,
  761 |             );
  762 |             const at = idx >= 0 ? idx : 0;
  763 |             const prevIdx = at - 1;
  764 | 
  765 |             if (prevIdx >= 0) {
  766 |               const t = hydrateTrack(s.queue[prevIdx], s.durationByRecordingId);
  767 |               return {
  768 |                 ...s,
  769 |                 current: t,
  770 |                 status: "loading",
  771 |                 loadingReason: "token",

  775 |                 pendingRecordingId: t.recordingId,

  781 |               const t = hydrateTrack(
  782 |                 s.queue[s.queue.length - 1],
  783 |                 s.durationByRecordingId,
  784 |               );

  786 |                 ...s,

  795 |               };
  796 |             }
  797 | 
  798 |             return {
  799 |               ...s,
  800 |               positionMs: 0,
  801 |               status: "loading",
  802 |               loadingReason: "attach",
  803 |               intent: "play",
  804 |               intentAtMs: Date.now(),
  805 |               pendingRecordingId: cur.recordingId,
  806 |               selectedRecordingId: cur.recordingId,
  807 |             };
  808 |           });
  809 |         });
  810 |       },
  811 | 
  812 |       setQueue: (tracks: PlayerTrack[], opts?: QueueContext) =>
  813 |         setState((s) => {
  814 |           const nextDurationByRecordingId = primeDurationByRecordingId(
  815 |             s.durationByRecordingId,
  816 |             tracks,
  817 |           );
  818 |           const nextAssetDurationByPlaybackId =
  819 |             retainAssetDurationsByPlaybackId(
  820 |               s.assetDurationByPlaybackId,
  821 |               tracks,
  822 |             );
  823 |           const hydratedQueue = hydrateTracks(
  824 |             tracks,
  825 |             nextDurationByRecordingId,
  826 |           );
  827 | 
  828 |           const initialCurrentRecordingId =

  831 |               : "";

  834 |             ? (hydratedQueue.find(

  836 |               ) ?? null)

  904 |               ? 0

  906 |                 ? s.positionMs

  911 |             pendingRecordingId: requestedInitialCurrent
  912 |               ? undefined
  913 |               : s.pendingRecordingId,
  914 |           };
  915 |         }),
  916 | 
  917 |       enqueue: (track: PlayerTrack) =>
  918 |         setState((s) => {
  919 |           const nextDurationByRecordingId = primeDurationByRecordingId(
  920 |             s.durationByRecordingId,
  921 |             [track],
  922 |           );
  923 |           const t = hydrateTrack(track, nextDurationByRecordingId);
  924 |           return {
  925 |             ...s,

  929 |             selectedRecordingId: s.selectedRecordingId ?? t.recordingId,
  930 |           };
  931 |         }),
  932 | 
  933 |       clearQueue: () =>
  934 |         setState((s) => ({
  935 |           ...s,
  936 |           queue: [],

  939 |           pendingRecordingId: undefined,

  947 |           queueSharePlaybackScopeId: null,
  948 |           assetDurationByPlaybackId: {},
  949 |         })),
  950 | 
  951 |       setPositionMs: (ms: number) =>
  952 |         setState((s) => ({
  953 |           ...s,
  954 |           positionMs: Math.max(0, ms),
  955 |         })),
  956 | 
  957 |       setDurationMs: (ms: number) =>
  958 |         setState((s) => {
  959 |           const cur = s.current;
  960 |           if (!cur) return s;
  961 |           if (!Number.isFinite(ms) || ms <= 0) return s;
  962 | 
  963 |           const alreadyCached =
  964 |             typeof s.durationByRecordingId[cur.recordingId] === "number" &&
  965 |             s.durationByRecordingId[cur.recordingId] > 0;
  966 |           const alreadyOnTrack =
  967 |             typeof cur.durationMs === "number" && cur.durationMs > 0;
  968 |           if (alreadyCached || alreadyOnTrack) return s;
  969 | 
  970 |           const nextDurationByRecordingId = {
  971 |             ...s.durationByRecordingId,
  972 |             [cur.recordingId]: ms,
  973 |           };
  974 | 
  975 |           const nextCurrent = { ...cur, durationMs: ms };

  977 |           const nextQueue = s.queue.map((t) => {
  978 |             if (t.recordingId !== cur.recordingId) return t;
  979 |             if (typeof t.durationMs === "number" && t.durationMs > 0) return t;
  980 |             changed = true;
  981 |             return { ...t, durationMs: ms };
  982 |           });
  983 | 
  984 |           return {
  985 |             ...s,
  986 |             durationByRecordingId: nextDurationByRecordingId,
  987 |             current: nextCurrent,
  988 |             queue: changed ? nextQueue : s.queue,
  989 |           };
  990 |         }),
  991 | 
  992 |       setAssetDurationMs: (playbackId: string, ms: number) => {
  993 |         const normalizedPlaybackId = playbackId.trim();

  995 | 
  996 |         if (!normalizedPlaybackId || normalizedDurationMs <= 0) return;

  998 |         setState((s) => {

 1012 |           };
 1013 |         });

 1016 |       setStatusExternal: (st: PlayerStatus) =>

 1018 |

 1024 |       seek: (ms: number) => {
 1025 |         setState((s) => {
 1026 |           const dur = getEffectiveDurationMs(s, s.current);
 1027 |           const next = dur > 0 ? clamp(ms, 0, dur) : Math.max(0, ms);
 1028 |           return {
 1029 |             ...s,
 1030 |             positionMs: next,
 1031 |             pendingSeekMs: next,

 1042 |         }),

 1044 |       setVolume: (v: number) =>
 1045 |         setState((s) => ({ ...s, volume: clamp(v, 0, 1) })),
 1046 |       toggleMute: () => setState((s) => ({ ...s, muted: !s.muted })),
 1047 | 
 1048 |       cycleRepeat: () =>
 1049 |         setState((s) => ({ ...s, repeat: nextRepeat(s.repeat) })),
 1050 | 
 1051 |       tick: (deltaMs: number) => {
 1052 |         setState((s) => {
 1053 |           if (s.status !== "playing") return s;
 1054 | 
 1055 |           const dur = getEffectiveDurationMs(s, s.current);
 1056 |           const nextPos = Math.max(0, s.positionMs + Math.max(0, deltaMs));
 1057 | 
 1058 |           if (dur <= 0) return { ...s, positionMs: nextPos };
 1059 |           if (nextPos < dur) return { ...s, positionMs: nextPos };
 1060 | 
 1061 |           if (s.repeat === "one") return { ...s, positionMs: 0 };
 1062 | 
 1063 |           const cur = s.current;
 1064 |           const idx = cur
 1065 |             ? s.queue.findIndex((t) => t.recordingId === cur.recordingId)
 1066 |             : -1;

 1068 |           const nextIdx = at + 1;
 1069 | 
 1070 |           if (nextIdx < s.queue.length) {
 1071 |             const t = hydrateTrack(s.queue[nextIdx], s.durationByRecordingId);
 1072 |             return {
 1073 |               ...s,
 1074 |               current: t,
 1075 |               positionMs: 0,
 1076 |               selectedRecordingId: t.recordingId,
 1077 |               pendingRecordingId: t.recordingId,
 1078 |             };
 1079 |           }
 1080 |

 1082 |             const t = hydrateTrack(s.queue[0], s.durationByRecordingId);
 1083 |             return {
 1084 |               ...s,
 1085 |               current: t,
 1086 |               positionMs: 0,
 1087 |               selectedRecordingId: t.recordingId,
 1088 |               pendingRecordingId: t.recordingId,
 1089 |             };
 1090 |           }

 1093 |         });
 1094 |       },
 1095 | 
 1096 |       clearError: () =>

 1098 | 
 1099 |       bumpReload: () => {

 1101 |           setState((s) => {
 1102 |             if (!s.current?.muxPlaybackId) {
 1103 |               return { ...s, reloadNonce: s.reloadNonce + 1 };
 1104 |             }
 1105 | 
 1106 |             return {
 1107 |               ...s,
 1108 |               reloadNonce: s.reloadNonce + 1,
 1109 |               status: "loading",

 1111 |               lastError: undefined,
 1112 |               intent: "play",
 1113 |               intentAtMs: Date.now(),
 1114 |               pendingRecordingId: s.current.recordingId,
 1115 |               selectedRecordingId: s.current.recordingId,
 1116 |             };
 1117 |           });
 1118 |         });
 1119 |       },
 1120 |     };
 1121 |   }, [guardPlayback]);
 1122 | 
 1123 |   const api: PlayerState & PlayerActions = React.useMemo(() => {
 1124 |     return {
 1125 |       ...state,
 1126 |       ...actions,
 1127 |     };
 1128 |   }, [state, actions]);
 1129 |

    7 | type SourceRect =
    8 |   | { mode: "full" }
    9 |   | { mode: "center"; scale?: number }
   10 |   | { mode: "random"; seed: number; scale?: number };
   11 | 
   12 | function clamp(n: number, a: number, b: number) {
   13 |   return Math.max(a, Math.min(b, n));
   14 | }
   15 | 
   16 | function pickRect(
   17 |   srcW: number,
   18 |   srcH: number,
   19 |   rect: SourceRect,
   20 | ): { sx: number; sy: number; sw: number; sh: number } {
   21 |   if (srcW <= 1 || srcH <= 1) return { sx: 0, sy: 0, sw: srcW, sh: srcH };
   22 |   if (rect.mode === "full") return { sx: 0, sy: 0, sw: srcW, sh: srcH };
   23 | 
   24 |   const scale = clamp(rect.scale ?? 0.55, 0.15, 1);
   25 |   const sw = Math.max(1, Math.floor(srcW * scale));
   26 |   const sh = Math.max(1, Math.floor(srcH * scale));
   27 | 
   28 |   if (rect.mode === "center") {
   29 |     const sx = Math.floor((srcW - sw) / 2);
   30 |     const sy = Math.floor((srcH - sh) / 2);
   31 |     return { sx, sy, sw, sh };
   32 |   }
   33 | 
   34 |   // deterministic “random”
   35 |   let x = Math.trunc(rect.seed);
   36 |   const rand = () => {
   37 |     // xorshift32
   38 |     x ^= x << 13;
   39 |     x ^= x >>> 17;
   40 |     x ^= x << 5;
   41 |     return ((x >>> 0) % 1_000_000) / 1_000_000;
   42 |   };
   43 | 
   44 |   const sx = Math.floor(rand() * Math.max(1, srcW - sw));
   45 |   const sy = Math.floor(rand() * Math.max(1, srcH - sh));
   46 |   return { sx, sy, sw, sh };
   47 | }
   48 | 
   49 | function cssBoxSize(el: HTMLCanvasElement): { cssW: number; cssH: number } {
   50 |   const cssW = Math.max(
   51 |     1,
   52 |     el.clientWidth || Math.round(el.getBoundingClientRect().width),
   53 |   );
   54 |   const cssH = Math.max(
   55 |     1,
   56 |     el.clientHeight || Math.round(el.getBoundingClientRect().height),
   57 |   );
   58 |   return { cssW, cssH };
   59 | }
   60 | 
   61 | function getSrcSize(src: HTMLCanvasElement): { srcW: number; srcH: number } {
   62 |   const srcW = src.width || src.clientWidth || 0;
   63 |   const srcH = src.height || src.clientHeight || 0;
   64 |   return { srcW, srcH };
   65 | }
   66 | 
   67 | function useVisualSnapshotSource() {
   68 |   const srcRef = React.useRef<HTMLCanvasElement | null>(null);
   69 |   const [hasSnapshotSource, setHasSnapshotSource] = React.useState(false);
   70 | 
   71 |   React.useEffect(() => {
   72 |     const apply = (canvas: HTMLCanvasElement | null) => {
   73 |       srcRef.current = canvas;
   74 | 
   75 |       const nextHasSnapshotSource = Boolean(canvas);
   76 | 
   77 |       setHasSnapshotSource((previous) =>
   78 |         previous === nextHasSnapshotSource ? previous : nextHasSnapshotSource,
   79 |       );
   80 |     };
   81 | 
   82 |     apply(visualSurface.getSnapshotCanvas());
   83 | 
   84 |     const unsubscribe = visualSurface.subscribe((event) => {
   85 |       if (event.type === "snapshot") {
   86 |         apply(event.canvas);
   87 |       }
   88 |     });
   89 | 
   90 |     return () => {
   91 |       try {
   92 |         unsubscribe();
   93 |       } catch {
   94 |         // VisualSurface subscriptions are best-effort decorative plumbing.
   95 |       }
   96 |     };
   97 |   }, []);
   98 | 
   99 |   return { srcRef, hasSnapshotSource };
  100 | }
  101 | 
  102 | function useCanvasRenderAvailability(
  103 |   canvasRef: React.RefObject<HTMLCanvasElement | null>,
  104 |   requestedActive: boolean,
  105 | ): boolean {
  106 |   const [documentVisible, setDocumentVisible] = React.useState(
  107 |     () => typeof document === "undefined" || !document.hidden,
  108 |   );
  109 |   const [intersecting, setIntersecting] = React.useState(true);
  110 | 
  111 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  112 |     const syncDocumentVisibility = () => {
  113 |       setDocumentVisible(!document.hidden);
  114 |     };
  115 | 
  116 |     syncDocumentVisibility();
  117 | 
  118 |     document.addEventListener(
  119 |       "visibilitychange",
  120 |       syncDocumentVisibility,
  121 |       false,
  122 |     );
  123 | 
  124 |     return () => {
  125 |       document.removeEventListener(
  126 |         "visibilitychange",
  127 |         syncDocumentVisibility,
  128 |         false,
  129 |       );
  130 |     };
  131 |   }, []);
  132 | 
  133 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  134 |     const canvas = canvasRef.current;
  135 | 
  136 |     if (!canvas || typeof IntersectionObserver === "undefined") {
  137 |       setIntersecting(true);
  138 |       return;
  139 |     }
  140 | 
  141 |     const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
  142 |       ([entry]) => {
  143 |         if (!entry) return;
  144 | 
  145 |         const nextIntersecting =
  146 |           entry.isIntersecting &&
  147 |           entry.intersectionRatio > 0 &&
  148 |           entry.boundingClientRect.width > 0 &&
  149 |           entry.boundingClientRect.height > 0;
  150 | 
  151 |         setIntersecting((previous) =>
  152 |           previous === nextIntersecting ? previous : nextIntersecting,
  153 |         );
  154 |       },
  155 |       { threshold: 0 },
  156 |     );
  157 | 
  158 |     observer.observe(canvas);
  159 | 
  160 |     return () => observer.disconnect();
  161 |   }, [canvasRef]);
  162 | 
  163 |   return requestedActive && documentVisible && intersecting;
  164 | }
  165 | 
  166 | /** Cover-fit mapping into destination (fills the canvas, cropping if needed). */
  167 | function coverMap(
  168 |   dstW: number,
  169 |   dstH: number,
  170 |   srcW: number,
  171 |   srcH: number,
  172 | ): { dX: number; dY: number; dW: number; dH: number } {
  173 |   const srcAspect = srcW / Math.max(1, srcH);
  174 |   const dstAspect = dstW / Math.max(1, dstH);
  175 | 
  176 |   let dW = dstW,
  177 |     dH = dstH,
  178 |     dX = 0,
  179 |     dY = 0;
  180 | 
  181 |   if (dstAspect > srcAspect) {
  182 |     dH = Math.round(dstW / srcAspect);
  183 |     dY = Math.round((dstH - dH) / 2);
  184 |   } else {
  185 |     dW = Math.round(dstH * srcAspect);
  186 |     dX = Math.round((dstW - dW) / 2);
  187 |   }
  188 | 
  189 |   return { dX, dY, dW, dH };
  190 | }

  192 | export function VisualizerSnapshotCanvas(props: {
  193 |   className?: string;
  194 |   style?: React.CSSProperties;
  195 |   fps?: number;
  196 |   opacity?: number;
  197 |   sourceRect?: SourceRect;
  198 |   active?: boolean;
  199 |   /** Optional canvas-side filter (avoids CSS filter compositor flicker) */
  200 |   ctxFilter?: string;
  201 | }) {
  202 |   const {
  203 |     className,
  204 |     style,
  205 |     fps = 14,
  206 |     opacity = 0.55,
  207 |     sourceRect = { mode: "center" },
  208 |     active = true,
  209 |     ctxFilter,
  210 |   } = props;
  211 | 
  212 |   const canvasRef = React.useRef<HTMLCanvasElement | null>(null);
  213 |   const { srcRef, hasSnapshotSource } = useVisualSnapshotSource();
  214 | 
  215 |   const shouldRender = useCanvasRenderAvailability(
  216 |     canvasRef,
  217 |     active && hasSnapshotSource,
  218 |   );
  219 | 
  220 |   // Keep latest prop values without restarting the visible render loop.
  221 |   const fpsRef = React.useRef(fps);
  222 |   const opacityRef = React.useRef(opacity);
  223 |   const sourceRectRef = React.useRef<SourceRect>(sourceRect);
  224 |   const ctxFilterRef = React.useRef<string | undefined>(ctxFilter);
  225 | 
  226 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  227 |     fpsRef.current = fps;
  228 |     opacityRef.current = opacity;
  229 |     sourceRectRef.current = sourceRect;
  230 |     ctxFilterRef.current = ctxFilter;
  231 |   }, [fps, opacity, sourceRect, ctxFilter]);
  232 | 
  233 |   // Canvas sizing via ResizeObserver (no per-frame layout reads).
  234 |   const sizeRef = React.useRef({ pxW: 1, pxH: 1, dpr: 1 });
  235 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  236 |     const dst = canvasRef.current;
  237 |     if (!dst) return;
  238 | 
  239 |     const apply = () => {
  240 |       const { cssW, cssH } = cssBoxSize(dst);
  241 |       const dpr = Math.max(1, Math.min(2.5, window.devicePixelRatio || 1));
  242 |       const pxW = Math.max(1, Math.round(cssW * dpr));
  243 |       const pxH = Math.max(1, Math.round(cssH * dpr));
  244 | 
  245 |       sizeRef.current = { pxW, pxH, dpr };
  246 |       if (dst.width !== pxW) dst.width = pxW;
  247 |       if (dst.height !== pxH) dst.height = pxH;
  248 |     };
  249 | 
  250 |     const ro = new ResizeObserver(apply);
  251 |     ro.observe(dst);
  252 |     apply();
  253 |     return () => ro.disconnect();
  254 |   }, []);
  255 | 
  256 |   // Draw only while the canvas is visible, active, and backed by a snapshot.
  257 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  258 |     if (!shouldRender) return;
  259 | 
  260 |     let raf: number | null = null;
  261 |     let timer: number | null = null;
  262 |     let lastDrawMs = 0;
  263 | 
  264 |     // Cache contexts + a backbuffer to avoid blanking on transient draw failures.
  265 |     const backRef = {
  266 |       canvas: null as HTMLCanvasElement | null,
  267 |       ctx: null as CanvasRenderingContext2D | null,
  268 |     };
  269 |     const ctxRef = { ctx: null as CanvasRenderingContext2D | null };
  270 | 
  271 |     const ensure2d = (
  272 |       c: HTMLCanvasElement,
  273 |     ): CanvasRenderingContext2D | null => {
  274 |       if (ctxRef.ctx && ctxRef.ctx.canvas === c) return ctxRef.ctx;
  275 |       ctxRef.ctx = c.getContext("2d", { alpha: true });
  276 |       return ctxRef.ctx;
  277 |     };
  278 | 
  279 |     const ensureBack = (
  280 |       w: number,
  281 |       h: number,
  282 |     ): CanvasRenderingContext2D | null => {
  283 |       let bc = backRef.canvas;
  284 |       if (!bc) {
  285 |         bc = document.createElement("canvas");
  286 |         backRef.canvas = bc;
  287 |       }
  288 |       if (bc.width !== w) bc.width = w;
  289 |       if (bc.height !== h) bc.height = h;
  290 | 
  291 |       if (backRef.ctx && backRef.ctx.canvas === bc) return backRef.ctx;
  292 |       backRef.ctx = bc.getContext("2d", { alpha: true });
  293 |       return backRef.ctx;
  294 |     };
  295 |     const scheduleNextFrame = () => {
  296 |       const interval = 1000 / Math.max(1, fpsRef.current);
  297 |       const elapsedMs = lastDrawMs ? performance.now() - lastDrawMs : interval;
  298 |       const delayMs = Math.max(0, interval - elapsedMs);
  299 | 
  300 |       const queueFrame = () => {
  301 |         timer = null;
  302 |         raf = window.requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  303 |       };
  304 | 
  305 |       if (delayMs <= 1) {
  306 |         queueFrame();
  307 |       } else {
  308 |         timer = window.setTimeout(queueFrame, delayMs);
  309 |       }
  310 |     };
  311 | 
  312 |     const tick = (t: number) => {
  313 |       raf = null;
  314 |       lastDrawMs = t;
  315 | 
  316 |       const dst = canvasRef.current;
  317 |       const src = srcRef.current;
  318 | 
  319 |       if (!dst || !src) {
  320 |         scheduleNextFrame();
  321 |         return;
  322 |       }
  323 | 
  324 |       const { pxW, pxH } = sizeRef.current;
  325 |       const ctx = ensure2d(dst);
  326 | 
  327 |       if (!ctx) {
  328 |         scheduleNextFrame();
  329 |         return;
  330 |       }
  331 | 
  332 |       const { srcW, srcH } = getSrcSize(src);
  333 | 
  334 |       if (srcW < 2 || srcH < 2) {
  335 |         scheduleNextFrame();
  336 |         return;
  337 |       }
  338 | 
  339 |       const bctx = ensureBack(pxW, pxH);
  340 | 
  341 |       if (!bctx || !backRef.canvas) {
  342 |         scheduleNextFrame();
  343 |         return;
  344 |       }
  345 | 
  346 |       const { sx, sy, sw, sh } = pickRect(srcW, srcH, sourceRectRef.current);
  347 |       const { dX, dY, dW, dH } = coverMap(pxW, pxH, sw, sh);
  348 | 
  349 |       try {
  350 |         // ---- render onto backbuffer ----
  351 |         bctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
  352 |         bctx.clearRect(0, 0, pxW, pxH);
  353 | 
  354 |         bctx.globalCompositeOperation = "source-over";
  355 |         bctx.globalAlpha = opacityRef.current;
  356 |         bctx.filter = ctxFilterRef.current ?? "none";
  357 |         bctx.drawImage(src, sx, sy, sw, sh, dX, dY, dW, dH);
  358 | 
  359 |         // ---- present once ----
  360 |         ctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
  361 |         ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  362 |         ctx.filter = "none";
  363 |         ctx.globalCompositeOperation = "copy";
  364 |         ctx.drawImage(backRef.canvas, 0, 0);
  365 |       } catch {
  366 |         // Keep the last good onscreen frame.
  367 |       }
  368 | 
  369 |       scheduleNextFrame();
  370 |     };
  371 | 
  372 |     scheduleNextFrame();
  373 | 
  374 |     return () => {
  375 |       if (raf != null) window.cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
  376 |       if (timer != null) window.clearTimeout(timer);
  377 |     };
  378 |   }, [shouldRender, srcRef]);
  379 | 
  380 |   return (
  381 |     <canvas
  382 |       ref={canvasRef}
  383 |       aria-hidden="true"
  384 |       className={className}
  385 |       style={{
  386 |         width: "100%",
  387 |         height: "100%",
  388 |         display: "block",
  389 |         pointerEvents: "none",
  390 |         background: "transparent",
  391 |         transform: "translateZ(0)",
  392 |         backfaceVisibility: "hidden",
  393 |         willChange: "transform, opacity",
  394 |         contain: "paint",
  395 |         ...style,
  396 |       }}
  397 |     />
  398 |   );
  399 | }

  401 | function VisualizerRingGlowCanvas(props: {
  402 |   size: number;
  403 |   ringPx: number;
  404 |   glowPx: number;
  405 |   blurPx: number;
  406 |   opacity: number;
  407 |   seed: number;
  408 |   fps: number;
  409 |   active: boolean;
  410 |   sourceRect: SourceRect;
  411 | }) {
  412 |   const { size, ringPx, glowPx, blurPx, opacity, fps, active, sourceRect } =
  413 |     props;
  414 | 
  415 |   const canvasRef = React.useRef<HTMLCanvasElement | null>(null);
  416 |   const { srcRef, hasSnapshotSource } = useVisualSnapshotSource();
  417 | 
  418 |   const shouldRender = useCanvasRenderAvailability(
  419 |     canvasRef,
  420 |     active && hasSnapshotSource,
  421 |   );
  422 | 
  423 |   // Keep latest prop values without restarting the visible render loop.
  424 |   const fpsRef = React.useRef(fps);
  425 |   const opacityRef = React.useRef(opacity);
  426 |   const blurRef = React.useRef(blurPx);
  427 |   const ringRef = React.useRef(ringPx);
  428 |   const glowRef = React.useRef(glowPx);
  429 |   const sizeParamRef = React.useRef(size);

  431 | 
  432 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  433 |     fpsRef.current = fps;
  434 |     opacityRef.current = opacity;
  435 |     blurRef.current = blurPx;
  436 |     ringRef.current = ringPx;
  437 |     glowRef.current = glowPx;
  438 |     sizeParamRef.current = size;
  439 |     sourceRectRef.current = sourceRect;
  440 |   }, [fps, opacity, blurPx, ringPx, glowPx, size, sourceRect]);
  441 | 
  442 |   // Size canvas via ResizeObserver on its own CSS box (explicit width/height set by parent).
  443 |   const sizeRef = React.useRef({ pxW: 1, pxH: 1, dpr: 1 });
  444 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  445 |     const dst = canvasRef.current;
  446 |     if (!dst) return;
  447 | 
  448 |     const apply = () => {
  449 |       const { cssW, cssH } = cssBoxSize(dst);
  450 |       const dpr = Math.max(1, Math.min(2.5, window.devicePixelRatio || 1));
  451 |       const pxW = Math.max(1, Math.round(cssW * dpr));
  452 |       const pxH = Math.max(1, Math.round(cssH * dpr));
  453 | 
  454 |       sizeRef.current = { pxW, pxH, dpr };
  455 |       if (dst.width !== pxW) dst.width = pxW;
  456 |       if (dst.height !== pxH) dst.height = pxH;
  457 |     };
  458 | 
  459 |     const ro = new ResizeObserver(apply);
  460 |     ro.observe(dst);
  461 |     apply();
  462 |     return () => ro.disconnect();
  463 |   }, []);
  464 | 
  465 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  466 |     if (!shouldRender) return;
  467 | 
  468 |     let raf: number | null = null;
  469 |     let timer: number | null = null;
  470 |     let lastDrawMs = 0;
  471 | 
  472 |     const backRef = {
  473 |       canvas: null as HTMLCanvasElement | null,
  474 |       ctx: null as CanvasRenderingContext2D | null,
  475 |     };
  476 |     const ctxRef = { ctx: null as CanvasRenderingContext2D | null };
  477 | 
  478 |     const ensure2d = (
  479 |       c: HTMLCanvasElement,
  480 |     ): CanvasRenderingContext2D | null => {
  481 |       if (ctxRef.ctx && ctxRef.ctx.canvas === c) return ctxRef.ctx;
  482 |       ctxRef.ctx = c.getContext("2d", { alpha: true });
  483 |       return ctxRef.ctx;
  484 |     };
  485 | 
  486 |     const ensureBack = (
  487 |       w: number,
  488 |       h: number,
  489 |     ): CanvasRenderingContext2D | null => {
  490 |       let bc = backRef.canvas;
  491 |       if (!bc) {
  492 |         bc = document.createElement("canvas");
  493 |         backRef.canvas = bc;
  494 |       }
  495 |       if (bc.width !== w) bc.width = w;
  496 |       if (bc.height !== h) bc.height = h;
  497 | 
  498 |       if (backRef.ctx && backRef.ctx.canvas === bc) return backRef.ctx;
  499 |       backRef.ctx = bc.getContext("2d", { alpha: true });
  500 |       return backRef.ctx;
  501 |     };
  502 | 
  503 |     const scheduleNextFrame = () => {
  504 |       const interval = 1000 / Math.max(1, fpsRef.current);
  505 |       const elapsedMs = lastDrawMs ? performance.now() - lastDrawMs : interval;
  506 |       const delayMs = Math.max(0, interval - elapsedMs);
  507 | 
  508 |       const queueFrame = () => {
  509 |         timer = null;
  510 |         raf = window.requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  511 |       };
  512 | 
  513 |       if (delayMs <= 1) {
  514 |         queueFrame();
  515 |       } else {
  516 |         timer = window.setTimeout(queueFrame, delayMs);
  517 |       }
  518 |     };
  519 | 
  520 |     const tick = (t: number) => {
  521 |       raf = null;
  522 |       lastDrawMs = t;
  523 | 
  524 |       const dst = canvasRef.current;
  525 |       const src = srcRef.current;
  526 | 
  527 |       if (!dst || !src) {
  528 |         scheduleNextFrame();
  529 |         return;
  530 |       }
  531 | 
  532 |       const { pxW, pxH, dpr } = sizeRef.current;
  533 |       const ctx = ensure2d(dst);
  534 | 
  535 |       if (!ctx) {
  536 |         scheduleNextFrame();
  537 |         return;
  538 |       }
  539 | 
  540 |       const { srcW, srcH } = getSrcSize(src);
  541 | 
  542 |       if (srcW < 2 || srcH < 2) {
  543 |         scheduleNextFrame();
  544 |         return;
  545 |       }
  546 | 
  547 |       const ringPxNow = ringRef.current;
  548 |       const glowPxNow = glowRef.current;
  549 |       const blurPxNow = blurRef.current;
  550 |       const sizeNow = sizeParamRef.current;
  551 | 
  552 |       // geometry (CSS px)
  553 |       const pad = ringPxNow + glowPxNow;
  554 |       const bleed = Math.max(2, Math.round(blurPxNow * 2));
  555 |       const outerPad = pad + bleed;
  556 | 
  557 |       const outerR = sizeNow / 2 + outerPad;
  558 |       const fadeWidth = glowPxNow + bleed;
  559 |       const fadeStart = Math.max(0, outerR - fadeWidth);
  560 |       const innerR = Math.max(0, sizeNow / 2 - ringPxNow);
  561 | 
  562 |       const { sx, sy, sw, sh } = pickRect(srcW, srcH, sourceRectRef.current);
  563 | 
  564 |       // cover fill: keep aspect, crop if needed
  565 |       const { dX, dY, dW, dH } = coverMap(pxW, pxH, sw, sh);
  566 | 
  567 |       const bctx = ensureBack(pxW, pxH);
  568 | 
  569 |       if (!bctx || !backRef.canvas) {
  570 |         scheduleNextFrame();
  571 |         return;
  572 |       }
  573 | 
  574 |       try {
  575 |         // ---- render EVERYTHING onto backbuffer ----
  576 |         bctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
  577 |         bctx.clearRect(0, 0, pxW, pxH);
  578 | 
  579 |         // texture
  580 |         bctx.save();
  581 |         bctx.globalAlpha = opacityRef.current;
  582 |         bctx.globalCompositeOperation = "screen";
  583 |         bctx.filter = `blur(${Math.max(0, blurPxNow) * dpr}px) contrast(1.55) saturate(1.55) brightness(1.25)`;
  584 |         bctx.drawImage(src, sx, sy, sw, sh, dX, dY, dW, dH);
  585 |         bctx.restore();
  586 | 
  587 |         // donut mask
  588 |         const cx = pxW / 2;
  589 |         const cy = pxH / 2;
  590 |         const outerRp = outerR * dpr;
  591 |         const innerRp = innerR * dpr;
  592 | 
  593 |         bctx.save();
  594 |         bctx.globalCompositeOperation = "destination-in";
  595 |         bctx.beginPath();
  596 |         bctx.arc(cx, cy, outerRp, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  597 |         bctx.closePath();
  598 |         bctx.fill();
  599 |         bctx.restore();
  600 | 
  601 |         if (innerRp > 0) {
  602 |           bctx.save();
  603 |           bctx.globalCompositeOperation = "destination-out";
  604 |           bctx.beginPath();
  605 |           bctx.arc(cx, cy, innerRp, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  606 |           bctx.closePath();
  607 |           bctx.fill();
  608 |           bctx.restore();
  609 |         }
  610 | 
  611 |         // outer fade
  612 |         bctx.save();
  613 |         bctx.globalCompositeOperation = "destination-in";
  614 |         const g = bctx.createRadialGradient(cx, cy, 0, cx, cy, outerRp);
  615 |         const s = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, fadeStart / outerR));
  616 |         g.addColorStop(0, "rgba(0,0,0,1)");
  617 |         g.addColorStop(s, "rgba(0,0,0,1)");
  618 |         g.addColorStop(1, "rgba(0,0,0,0)");
  619 |         bctx.fillStyle = g;
  620 |         bctx.fillRect(0, 0, pxW, pxH);
  621 |         bctx.restore();
  622 | 
  623 |         // ---- present once ----
  624 |         ctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
  625 |         ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  626 |         ctx.globalCompositeOperation = "copy";
  627 |         ctx.drawImage(backRef.canvas, 0, 0);
  628 |       } catch {
  629 |         // Keep the last good onscreen frame.
  630 |       }
  631 | 
  632 |       scheduleNextFrame();
  633 |     };
  634 | 
  635 |     scheduleNextFrame();
  636 | 
  637 |     return () => {
  638 |       if (raf != null) window.cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
  639 |       if (timer != null) window.clearTimeout(timer);
  640 |     };
  641 |   }, [shouldRender, srcRef]);
  642 | 
  643 |   // CSS size is controlled by parent, but we set explicit dimensions for clarity
  644 |   const pad = ringPx + glowPx;
  645 |   const bleed = Math.max(2, Math.round(blurPx * 2));
  646 |   const outerPad = pad + bleed;
  647 |   const cssSize = size + outerPad * 2;
  648 | 
  649 |   return (
  650 |     <canvas
  651 |       ref={canvasRef}
  652 |       aria-hidden="true"
  653 |       style={{
  654 |         width: cssSize,
  655 |         height: cssSize,
  656 |         display: "block",
  657 |         pointerEvents: "none",
  658 |         background: "transparent",
  659 |       }}
  660 |     />
  661 |   );
  662 | }

===== COMPLETE FILE: app/home/player/lyrics/ensureLyricsForTrack.ts =====
    1 | // web/app/home/player/lyrics/ensureLyricsForTrack.ts
    2 | "use client";
    3 | 
    4 | import { lyricsSurface } from "./lyricsSurface";
    5 | import { fetchLyricsByrecordingId } from "./fetchLyricsByRecordingId";
    6 | import type { AlbumLyricsBundle } from "@/lib/types";
    7 | 
    8 | // Module-scope in-flight map so *any* caller (any surface) dedupes fetches.
    9 | const inFlight = new Map<string, AbortController>();
   10 | 
   11 | function hasOwn(obj: object, key: string): boolean {
   12 |   return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(obj, key);
   13 | }
   14 | 
   15 | /**
   16 |  * Prime lyricsSurface from an album-provided bundle.
   17 |  * - Merges without clobbering existing per-track entries (unless missing).
   18 |  * - Ensures we still preserve "known no lyrics" as [] if already present.
   19 |  * - No network; safe to call repeatedly (idempotent-ish).
   20 |  */
   21 | export function primeLyricsFromAlbumBundle(
   22 |   bundle: AlbumLyricsBundle | null | undefined,
   23 | ) {
   24 |   if (!bundle) return;
   25 | 
   26 |   const snap = lyricsSurface.getSnapshot();
   27 | 
   28 |   const cuesIn = bundle.cuesByRecordingId ?? {};
   29 |   const offIn = bundle.offsetByRecordingId ?? {};
   30 | 
   31 |   // Build merged maps but only add keys that are currently unknown.
   32 |   let cuesOut = snap.cuesByRecordingId;
   33 |   let offOut = snap.offsetByRecordingId;
   34 | 
   35 |   for (const [recordingIdRaw, cuesRaw] of Object.entries(cuesIn)) {
   36 |     const id = (recordingIdRaw ?? "").trim();
   37 |     if (!id) continue;
   38 | 
   39 |     if (hasOwn(cuesOut, id)) continue; // don't overwrite existing (including [] known-no-lyrics)
   40 | 
   41 |     const cues = Array.isArray(cuesRaw) ? cuesRaw : [];
   42 |     cuesOut = { ...cuesOut, [id]: cues };
   43 |   }
   44 | 
   45 |   for (const [recordingIdRaw, offRaw] of Object.entries(offIn)) {
   46 |     const id = (recordingIdRaw ?? "").trim();
   47 |     if (!id) continue;
   48 | 
   49 |     if (hasOwn(offOut, id)) continue; // don't overwrite existing
   50 | 
   51 |     const n =
   52 |       typeof offRaw === "number" && Number.isFinite(offRaw)
   53 |         ? Math.floor(offRaw)
   54 |         : 0;
   55 | 
   56 |     offOut = { ...offOut, [id]: n };
   57 |   }
   58 | 
   59 |   // Only write if something changed (cheap reference check).
   60 |   if (cuesOut === snap.cuesByRecordingId && offOut === snap.offsetByRecordingId) return;
   61 | 
   62 |   lyricsSurface.setMaps({
   63 |     cuesByrecordingId: cuesOut,
   64 |     offsetByrecordingId: offOut,
   65 |     globalOffsetMs: snap.globalOffsetMs,
   66 |   });
   67 | }
   68 | 
   69 | /**
   70 |  * Ensure lyrics (cues + offset) exist in lyricsSurface for recordingId.
   71 |  * - Dedupes concurrent calls (module-level inFlight).
   72 |  * - Caches "no lyrics" as [] to avoid refetch loops.
   73 |  * - Safe to call repeatedly from any surface.
   74 |  */
   75 | export async function ensureLyricsForTrack(
   76 |   recordingId: string,
   77 |   opts?: { signal?: AbortSignal },
   78 | ): Promise<void> {
   79 |   const id = (recordingId ?? "").trim();
   80 |   if (!id) return;
   81 | 
   82 |   const snap = lyricsSurface.getSnapshot();
   83 |   const existing = snap.cuesByRecordingId[id];
   84 | 
   85 |   const knownKey = hasOwn(snap.cuesByRecordingId, id);
   86 |   const hasCues = Array.isArray(existing) && existing.length > 0;
   87 |   const knownNoLyrics =
   88 |     knownKey && Array.isArray(existing) && existing.length === 0;
   89 | 
   90 |   if (hasCues || knownNoLyrics) return;
   91 | 
   92 |   // Already fetching?
   93 |   if (inFlight.has(id)) return;
   94 | 
   95 |   const ac = new AbortController();
   96 |   inFlight.set(id, ac);
   97 | 
   98 |   // Caller abort should NOT abort the shared in-flight request.
   99 |   // It only means "this caller no longer cares". The module-scope fetch may be
  100 |   // servicing other callers (PlayerState, StageInline, etc).
  101 |   const outerSignal = opts?.signal;
  102 |   if (outerSignal?.aborted) {
  103 |     inFlight.delete(id);
  104 |     return;
  105 |   }
  106 | 
  107 |   try {
  108 |     const r = await fetchLyricsByrecordingId(id, ac.signal);
  109 |     if (!r) return;
  110 |     if (r.recordingId !== id) return;
  111 | 
  112 |     const prev = lyricsSurface.getSnapshot();
  113 | 
  114 |     const nextCuesByrecordingId =
  115 |       Array.isArray(r.cues) && r.cues.length
  116 |         ? { ...prev.cuesByRecordingId, [id]: r.cues }
  117 |         : hasOwn(prev.cuesByRecordingId, id)
  118 |           ? prev.cuesByRecordingId
  119 |           : { ...prev.cuesByRecordingId, [id]: [] };
  120 | 
  121 |     const nextOffsetByrecordingId =
  122 |       typeof r.offsetMs === "number" && Number.isFinite(r.offsetMs)
  123 |         ? { ...prev.offsetByRecordingId, [id]: r.offsetMs }
  124 |         : prev.offsetByRecordingId;
  125 | 
  126 |     lyricsSurface.setMaps({
  127 |       cuesByrecordingId: nextCuesByrecordingId,
  128 |       offsetByrecordingId: nextOffsetByrecordingId,
  129 |       // preserve whatever globalOffsetMs currently is (lyricsSurface handles defaulting)
  130 |       globalOffsetMs: prev.globalOffsetMs,
  131 |     });
  132 |   } finally {
  133 |     inFlight.delete(id);
  134 |   }
  135 | }

===== COMPLETE FILE: app/home/player/lyrics/lyricsSurface.ts =====
    1 | // web/app/home/player/lyrics/lyricsSurface.ts
    2 | "use client";
    3 | 
    4 | import type { LyricCue } from "@/lib/types";
    5 | 
    6 | export type CuesByrecordingId = Record<string, LyricCue[]>;
    7 | export type OffsetByrecordingId = Record<string, number>;
    8 | 
    9 | type Snapshot = {
   10 |   cuesByRecordingId: CuesByrecordingId;
   11 |   offsetByRecordingId: OffsetByrecordingId;
   12 |   globalOffsetMs: number;
   13 | };
   14 | 
   15 | type Listener = () => void;
   16 | 
   17 | let snap: Snapshot = {
   18 |   cuesByRecordingId: {},
   19 |   offsetByRecordingId: {},
   20 |   globalOffsetMs: 0,
   21 | };
   22 | 
   23 | const listeners = new Set<Listener>();
   24 | 
   25 | function emit() {
   26 |   for (const fn of listeners) fn();
   27 | }
   28 | 
   29 | export const lyricsSurface = {
   30 |   getSnapshot(): Snapshot {
   31 |     return snap;
   32 |   },
   33 | 
   34 |   subscribe(fn: Listener): () => void {
   35 |     listeners.add(fn);
   36 |     return () => listeners.delete(fn);
   37 |   },
   38 | 
   39 |   setMaps(next: {
   40 |     cuesByrecordingId?: CuesByrecordingId | null;
   41 |     offsetByrecordingId?: OffsetByrecordingId | null;
   42 |     globalOffsetMs?: number | null;
   43 |   }) {
   44 |     const cuesByRecordingId = next.cuesByrecordingId ?? {};
   45 |     const offsetByRecordingId = next.offsetByrecordingId ?? {};
   46 |     const globalOffsetMs =
   47 |       typeof next.globalOffsetMs === "number" && Number.isFinite(next.globalOffsetMs)
   48 |         ? next.globalOffsetMs
   49 |         : 0;
   50 | 
   51 |     // cheap identity guard: only emit if something actually changes by reference/value
   52 |     const changed =
   53 |       snap.cuesByRecordingId !== cuesByRecordingId ||
   54 |       snap.offsetByRecordingId !== offsetByRecordingId ||
   55 |       snap.globalOffsetMs !== globalOffsetMs;
   56 | 
   57 |     if (!changed) return;
   58 | 
   59 |     snap = { cuesByRecordingId, offsetByRecordingId, globalOffsetMs };
   60 |     emit();
   61 |   },
   62 | };

   60 | function getFreshCachedPlaybackAccessDecision(

   79 | function normalizeShareToken(value: string | null): string | null {
   80 |   const token = (value ?? "").trim();

   84 | function playbackAccessKey(request: PlaybackAccessRequest): string {
   85 |   const catalogueId = normalizeCatalogueId(request.catalogueId);
   86 |   const shareToken = normalizeShareToken(request.shareToken);
   87 | 
   88 |   return `${catalogueId}::st=${shareToken ?? ""}::identity=${

   90 |   }`;

   93 | function parseShareTokenAccess(
   94 |   value: unknown,
   95 | ): PlaybackShareTokenAccess | null {
   96 |   if (!value || typeof value !== "object") return null;
   97 | 
   98 |   const raw = value as Record<string, unknown>;
   99 |   const hasExpiresAt = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(raw, "expiresAt");
  100 |   const hasMaxRedemptions = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(
  101 |     raw,
  102 |     "maxRedemptions",
  103 |   );
  104 | 
  105 |   if (!hasExpiresAt && !hasMaxRedemptions) return null;
  106 | 
  107 |   const expiresAtRaw = raw.expiresAt;
  108 |   const maxRedemptionsRaw = raw.maxRedemptions;
  109 | 
  110 |   const expiresAt =
  111 |     expiresAtRaw == null
  112 |       ? null
  113 |       : typeof expiresAtRaw === "string" &&
  114 |           Number.isFinite(Date.parse(expiresAtRaw))
  115 |         ? expiresAtRaw
  116 |         : null;
  117 | 
  118 |   const maxRedemptions =
  119 |     maxRedemptionsRaw == null
  120 |       ? null
  121 |       : typeof maxRedemptionsRaw === "number" &&
  122 |           Number.isFinite(maxRedemptionsRaw) &&
  123 |           maxRedemptionsRaw >= 1
  124 |         ? Math.floor(maxRedemptionsRaw)
  125 |         : null;
  126 | 
  127 |   if (expiresAtRaw != null && expiresAt == null) return null;
  128 |   if (maxRedemptionsRaw != null && maxRedemptions == null) return null;
  129 | 
  130 |   return { expiresAt, maxRedemptions };
  131 | }
  132 | 
  133 | function parseSharePlaybackContext(value: unknown): string | null {
  134 |   if (typeof value !== "string") return null;
  135 | 
  136 |   const context = value.trim();
  137 | 
  138 |   return context.startsWith("stpc1.") && context.length <= 900 ? context : null;
  139 | }

  141 | function parseSharePlaybackScopeId(value: unknown): string | null {
  142 |   if (typeof value !== "string") return null;
  143 | 
  144 |   const scopeId = value.trim();
  145 | 
  146 |   return /^alb:[^\s]+$/i.test(scopeId) ? scopeId : null;
  147 | }

  158 | export function readPlaybackShareTokenFromLocation(): string | null {
  159 |   if (typeof window === "undefined") return null;

  167 | export function getCachedPlaybackAccessDecision(
  168 |   request: PlaybackAccessRequest,
  169 | ): PlaybackAccessDecision | null {
  170 |   return getFreshCachedPlaybackAccessDecision(playbackAccessKey(request));
  171 | }
  172 | 
  173 | export async function fetchPlaybackAccessDecision(
  174 |   request: PlaybackAccessRequest,
  175 | ): Promise<PlaybackAccessDecision> {
  176 |   const catalogueId = normalizeCatalogueId(request.catalogueId);
  177 | 
  178 |   if (!catalogueId) {
  179 |     throw new Error("Playback access requires a catalogue ID.");
  180 |   }
  181 | 
  182 |   const shareToken = normalizeShareToken(request.shareToken);
  183 |   const key = playbackAccessKey({
  184 |     catalogueId,
  185 |     shareToken,
  186 |     accessIdentityKey: request.accessIdentityKey,
  187 |   });
  188 | 
  189 |   const cached = getFreshCachedPlaybackAccessDecision(key);
  190 |   if (cached) return cached;
  191 | 
  192 |   const existing = inFlightByKey.get(key);
  193 |   if (existing) return existing;
  194 | 
  195 |   const promise = (async () => {
  196 |     const url = new URL("/api/access/check", window.location.origin);
  197 |     url.searchParams.set("albumId", catalogueId);
  198 | 
  199 |     if (shareToken) {
  200 |       url.searchParams.set("st", shareToken);
  201 |     }
  202 | 
  203 |     const response = await fetch(url.toString(), {
  204 |       method: "GET",
  205 |       cache: "no-store",
  206 |     });
  207 | 
  208 |     const body = (await response.json()) as PlaybackAccessResponse;
  209 | 
  210 |     const decision: PlaybackAccessDecision = {
  211 |       forCatalogueId: catalogueId,
  212 |       allowed: body.allowed !== false,
  213 |       embargoed: body.embargoed === true,
  214 |       releaseAt: body.releaseAt ?? null,
  215 |       code:
  216 |         typeof body.code === "string" && body.code.trim()
  217 |           ? body.code
  218 |           : undefined,
  219 |       action: isPlaybackAccessAction(body.action) ? body.action : undefined,
  220 |       reason:
  221 |         typeof body.reason === "string" && body.reason.trim()
  222 |           ? body.reason
  223 |           : undefined,
  224 |       corr: response.headers.get("x-correlation-id") ?? null,
  225 |       shareTokenAccess: parseShareTokenAccess(body.shareTokenAccess),
  226 |       sharePlaybackContext: parseSharePlaybackContext(
  227 |         body.sharePlaybackContext,
  228 |       ),
  229 |       sharePlaybackScopeId: parseSharePlaybackScopeId(
  230 |         body.sharePlaybackScopeId,
  231 |       ),
  232 |     };
  233 | 
  234 |     decisionsByKey.set(key, {
  235 |       decision,
  236 |       expiresAtMs: Date.now() + PLAYBACK_ACCESS_CACHE_TTL_MS,
  237 |     });
  238 | 
  239 |     return decision;
  240 |   })().finally(() => {
  241 |     inFlightByKey.delete(key);
  242 |   });
  243 | 
  244 |   inFlightByKey.set(key, promise);
  245 |   return promise;
  246 | }

   14 | function clean(s?: string | null) {
   15 |   const t = (s ?? "").toString().trim();
   16 |   return t.length ? t : undefined;
   17 | }

   19 | type AlbumResolveOk = {
   20 |   ok: true;
   21 |   album: {
   22 |     id: string;
   23 |     catalogueId: string | null;

   25 |     // `displayTitle` should be the human/stylised title (e.g. "GOD DEFEND").

   37 | function trimTrailingSlashes(value: string): string {
   38 |   let end = value.length;
   39 |   while (end > 0 && value.charCodeAt(end - 1) === 47) {
   40 |     end -= 1;
   41 |   }
   42 |   return value.slice(0, end);
   43 | }
   44 | 
   45 | async function resolveAlbumMeta(
   46 |   ctx: PlayerShareContext,
   47 |   opts?: { origin?: string },
   48 | ): Promise<
   49 |   Required<Pick<PlayerShareContext, "albumSlug">> & PlayerShareContext
   50 | > {
   51 |   const existingSlug = clean(ctx.albumSlug);
   52 |   if (existingSlug) return { ...ctx, albumSlug: existingSlug };
   53 | 
   54 |   const albumId = clean(ctx.albumId);
   55 |   if (!albumId) {
   56 |     throw new Error("Cannot share: missing albumSlug and albumId.");
   57 |   }
   58 | 
   59 |   const base = clean(opts?.origin);
   60 |   const trimmedBase = base ? trimTrailingSlashes(base) : "";
   61 | 
   62 |   const url = trimmedBase
   63 |     ? `${trimmedBase}/api/albums/by-id?albumId=${encodeURIComponent(albumId)}`
   64 |     : `/api/albums/by-id?albumId=${encodeURIComponent(albumId)}`;
   65 | 
   66 |   const res = await fetch(url, { method: "GET" });
   67 |   const data = (await res
   68 |     .json()
   69 |     .catch(() => null)) as AlbumResolveResponse | null;
   70 | 
   71 |   if (!res.ok || !data || data.ok !== true) {
   72 |     const msg =
   73 |       data && data.ok === false
   74 |         ? data.error
   75 |         : `Album resolve failed (${res.status})`;
   76 |     throw new Error(`Cannot share: ${msg}`);
   77 |   }
   78 | 
   79 |   const resolvedSlug = clean(data.album.slug);
   80 |   if (!resolvedSlug)
   81 |     throw new Error("Cannot share: resolved album is missing slug.");
   82 | 
   83 |   const resolvedTitle =
   84 |     clean(data.album.displayTitle) ?? clean(data.album.title);
   85 | 
   86 |   return {
   87 |     ...ctx,
   88 |     albumSlug: resolvedSlug,
   89 |     albumTitle: clean(ctx.albumTitle) ?? resolvedTitle,
   90 |     albumArtist: clean(ctx.albumArtist) ?? clean(data.album.artist),
   91 |   };
   92 | }

    1 | // web/app/home/player/visualSurface.ts
    2 | "use client";
    3 | 
    4 | import type { StageVariant } from "./mediaSurface";
    5 | 
    6 | export type VisualSurfaceEvent =
    7 |   | { type: "canvas"; canvas: HTMLCanvasElement | null }
    8 |   | { type: "snapshot"; canvas: HTMLCanvasElement | null };
    9 | 
   10 | type Listener = (e: VisualSurfaceEvent) => void;
   11 | 
   12 | /** Toggle via: window.__AF_VIS_DEBUG = true */
   13 | function debugEnabled(): boolean {
   14 |   return Boolean((globalThis as { __AF_VIS_DEBUG?: boolean }).__AF_VIS_DEBUG);
   15 | }
   16 | 
   17 | class VisualSurface {
   18 |   private listeners = new Set<Listener>();
   19 | 
   20 |   private inlineCanvas: HTMLCanvasElement | null = null;
   21 |   private fullscreenCanvas: HTMLCanvasElement | null = null;
   22 |   private active: HTMLCanvasElement | null = null;
   23 | 
   24 |   private inlineSnapshotCanvas: HTMLCanvasElement | null = null;
   25 |   private fullscreenSnapshotCanvas: HTMLCanvasElement | null = null;
   26 |   private activeSnapshot: HTMLCanvasElement | null = null;
   27 | 
   28 |   private notify(e: VisualSurfaceEvent) {
   29 |     for (const fn of this.listeners) {
   30 |       try {
   31 |         fn(e);
   32 |       } catch {
   33 |         // ignore listener errors
   34 |       }
   35 |     }
   36 |   }
   37 | 
   38 |   private recompute() {
   39 |     const nextCanvas = this.fullscreenCanvas ?? this.inlineCanvas ?? null;
   40 |     const nextSnapshot =
   41 |       this.fullscreenSnapshotCanvas ?? this.inlineSnapshotCanvas ?? null;
   42 | 
   43 |     const canvasChanged = nextCanvas !== this.active;
   44 |     const snapshotChanged = nextSnapshot !== this.activeSnapshot;
   45 | 
   46 |     if (!canvasChanged && !snapshotChanged) return;
   47 | 
   48 |     this.active = nextCanvas;
   49 |     this.activeSnapshot = nextSnapshot;
   50 | 
   51 |     if (canvasChanged) {
   52 |       this.notify({ type: "canvas", canvas: this.active });
   53 |     }
   54 | 
   55 |     if (snapshotChanged) {
   56 |       this.notify({ type: "snapshot", canvas: this.activeSnapshot });
   57 |     }
   58 | 
   59 |     if (debugEnabled()) {
   60 |       console.log("[vis] recompute", {
   61 |         activeVariant: this.fullscreenCanvas
   62 |           ? "fullscreen"
   63 |           : this.inlineCanvas
   64 |             ? "inline"
   65 |             : null,
   66 |         hasCanvas: Boolean(this.active),
   67 |         hasSnapshot: Boolean(this.activeSnapshot),
   68 |       });
   69 |     }
   70 |   }
   71 | 
   72 |   getCanvas(): HTMLCanvasElement | null {
   73 |     return this.active;
   74 |   }
   75 | 
   76 |   getSnapshotCanvas(): HTMLCanvasElement | null {
   77 |     return this.activeSnapshot;
   78 |   }
   79 | 
   80 |   /**
   81 |    * Register live and snapshot canvases for a stage variant. Fullscreen always wins if present.
   82 |    * Returns an unsubscribe cleanup.
   83 |    */
   84 |   registerCanvas(
   85 |     variant: StageVariant,
   86 |     canvas: HTMLCanvasElement | null,
   87 |     snapshotCanvas?: HTMLCanvasElement | null,
   88 |   ) {
   89 |     if (variant === "fullscreen") {
   90 |       this.fullscreenCanvas = canvas;
   91 |       this.fullscreenSnapshotCanvas = snapshotCanvas ?? null;
   92 |     } else {
   93 |       this.inlineCanvas = canvas;
   94 |       this.inlineSnapshotCanvas = snapshotCanvas ?? null;
   95 |     }
   96 | 
   97 |     this.recompute();
   98 | 
   99 |     return () => {
  100 |       if (variant === "fullscreen") {
  101 |         if (this.fullscreenCanvas === canvas) this.fullscreenCanvas = null;
  102 |         if (this.fullscreenSnapshotCanvas === (snapshotCanvas ?? null)) {
  103 |           this.fullscreenSnapshotCanvas = null;
  104 |         }
  105 |       } else {
  106 |         if (this.inlineCanvas === canvas) this.inlineCanvas = null;
  107 |         if (this.inlineSnapshotCanvas === (snapshotCanvas ?? null)) {
  108 |           this.inlineSnapshotCanvas = null;
  109 |         }
  110 |       }
  111 |       this.recompute();
  112 |     };
  113 |   }
  114 | 
  115 |   subscribe(fn: Listener) {
  116 |     this.listeners.add(fn);
  117 | 
  118 |     fn({ type: "canvas", canvas: this.active });
  119 |     fn({ type: "snapshot", canvas: this.activeSnapshot });
  120 | 
  121 |     return () => {
  122 |       this.listeners.delete(fn);
  123 |     };
  124 |   }
  125 | }
  126 | 
  127 | export const visualSurface = new VisualSurface();

    8 | // Secondary query policy (strict allowlist).
    9 | // Surfaces are path-based now; query is for secondary concerns only.
   10 | const ALLOW_KEYS = new Set([
   11 |   "st",
   12 |   "share",
   13 |   "autoplay",
   14 |   "gift",
   15 |   "checkout",
   16 |   "purchase",
   17 |   "purchaseAlbum",

   19 |   "pt",

   22 | const ALLOW_PREFIXES = ["utm_"];
   23 | 
   24 | // Secondary query keys that should survive internal navigation.
   25 | // `st` is durable access context for share-token playback rehydration.
   26 | const PERSIST_KEYS = new Set(["st"]);
   27 | 
   28 | // Explicitly forbidden legacy/state keys (must never persist).
   29 | const FORBIDDEN_KEYS = new Set(["p", "album", "track", "t"]);

   32 |   return pathname === "/journal" || pathname.startsWith("/journal/");
   33 | }

   38 | }

   41 |   if (!isJournalPath(pathname)) params.delete("post");

   49 | function isAllowedKey(k: string): boolean {
   50 |   if (ALLOW_KEYS.has(k)) return true;
   51 |   return ALLOW_PREFIXES.some((p) => k.startsWith(p));
   52 | }

   54 | /** Mutates params to enforce the secondary-query policy. */
   55 | export function sanitizeSecondaryQuery(params: URLSearchParams): void {
   56 |   sanitizeSecondaryQueryForPath(params, safeGetPathname());
   57 | }
   58 | 
   59 | /** Mutates params to enforce the secondary-query policy for a destination path. */
   60 | export function sanitizeSecondaryQueryForPath(
   61 |   params: URLSearchParams,
   62 |   pathname: string,
   63 | ): void {
   64 |   // 1) kill forbidden keys always
   65 |   for (const k of Array.from(params.keys())) {
   66 |     if (FORBIDDEN_KEYS.has(k)) params.delete(k);
   67 |   }
   68 | 
   69 |   // 2) strict allowlist for everything else
   70 |   for (const k of Array.from(params.keys())) {
   71 |     if (!isAllowedKey(k)) params.delete(k);
   72 |   }
   73 | 
   74 |   // 3) normalize share -> st when present (prefer explicit st)
   75 |   const st = (params.get("st") ?? "").trim();
   76 |   const share = (params.get("share") ?? "").trim();
   77 |   if (!st && share) params.set("st", share);
   78 |   if (params.has("share")) params.delete("share");
   79 | 
   80 |   // 4) remove query keys that only make sense on specific routes
   81 |   stripPathScopedKeys(params, pathname);
   82 | 
   83 |   // 5) trim empties
   84 |   for (const [k, v] of Array.from(params.entries())) {
   85 |     if (!String(v ?? "").trim()) params.delete(k);
   86 |   }
   87 | }
   88 |

  115 | /**
  116 |  * Appends durable secondary query state to same-origin internal hrefs.
  117 |  *
  118 |  * Preserves hash fragments, so:
  119 |  *   /exegesis/foo#l=bar
  120 |  * becomes:
  121 |  *   /exegesis/foo?st=TOKEN#l=bar
  122 |  */
  123 | export function appendPersistentSecondaryQueryToHref(href: string): string {
  124 |   if (typeof window === "undefined") return href;
  125 | 
  126 |   const persistent = pickPersistentSecondaryQueryFromLocation();
  127 |   if (Array.from(persistent.keys()).length === 0) return href;
  128 | 
  129 |   const url = new URL(href, window.location.href);
  130 |   if (url.origin !== window.location.origin) return href;
  131 | 
  132 |   const params = new URLSearchParams(url.search);
  133 |   sanitizeSecondaryQueryForPath(params, url.pathname);
  134 | 
  135 |   for (const [k, v] of persistent.entries()) {
  136 |     if (!params.has(k)) params.set(k, v);
  137 |   }
  138 | 
  139 |   sanitizeSecondaryQueryForPath(params, url.pathname);
  140 | 
  141 |   const next = params.toString();
  142 |   url.search = next ? `?${next}` : "";
  143 | 
  144 |   return `${url.pathname}${url.search}${url.hash}`;
  145 | }

  147 | export function useClientSearchParams(): URLSearchParams {
  148 |   const [qs, setQs] = React.useState<string>(() => safeGetSearch());
  149 | 
  150 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  151 |     const onPop = () => setQs(safeGetSearch());
  152 |     const onCustom = () => setQs(safeGetSearch());
  153 | 
  154 |     window.addEventListener("popstate", onPop);
  155 |     window.addEventListener(QS_EVENT, onCustom as EventListener);
  156 |     return () => {
  157 |       window.removeEventListener("popstate", onPop);
  158 |       window.removeEventListener(QS_EVENT, onCustom as EventListener);
  159 |     };
  160 |   }, []);
  161 | 
  162 |   return React.useMemo(() => {
  163 |     const params = new URLSearchParams((qs || "").replace(/^\?/, ""));
  164 |     sanitizeSecondaryQuery(params);
  165 |     return params;
  166 |   }, [qs]);
  167 | }

  171 |   return v === "1" || v === "true" || v === "yes";
  172 | }
